### [105] [Unlocking the Wronskian Tower: A Simplification of the Holomorphic Modular Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18375)
*Arpit Das,Sunil Mukhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了联系不同Wronskian指标ℓ的微分算子，使得高ℓ解可归约到低ℓ解，从而高效构造有理共形场论（RCFT）的特征标与准特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接求解模线性微分方程（MLDE）来分类RCFT特征标在高ℓ时因可动极点和附属参数而变得困难，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 构造了保持模协变性和q级数整性的微分算子，将高ℓ解映射到低ℓ解；在秩2情形从Mathur–Mukhi–Sen方程生成所有Wronskian扇区；在秩3及以上将高ℓ准特征标的构造简化为更低ℓ的简单方程。

Result: 在秩2中验证了ℓ=2准特征标的符号猜想；建立了一种跨Wronskian塔组织候选RCFT特征标与准特征标的新路径。

Conclusion: 该方法为系统分类RCFT特征标及准特征标提供了一种高效的新途径，克服了高ℓ直接求解MLDE的困难。

Abstract: Characters of rational conformal field theories solve modular linear differential equations labelled by their order and the Wronskian index $\ell$. Direct classification of admissible solutions by solving MLDEs becomes increasingly difficult at higher $\ell$ -- where movable poles and accessory parameters appear. In this work we introduce differential operators that relate higher-$\ell$ solutions to lower-$\ell$ ones while preserving modular covariance and integrality of the \(q\)-series. In rank two, this generates all allowed Wronskian sectors from the Mathur--Mukhi--Sen equation. In rank three and higher, it reduces the construction of higher-$\ell$ quasi-characters to simpler equations with lower $\ell$. This gives an efficient new route for organising candidate RCFT characters, and more generally quasi-characters, across the Wronskian tower. As an application, we apply our construction to prove a previously conjectured property on the signs of $\ell=2$ quasi-characters in rank 2.

</details>


### [106] [The black hole S-matrix in gauge/gravity duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18393)
*Emil J. Martinec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用规范/引力对偶的库仑支特性，构建了一个S矩阵：初始态为膜壳汇聚于库仑支原点，中间态为长寿命黑洞，最终通过慢速辐射膜退回库仑支。文中比较了体有效场论与对偶规范理论对该过程的描述，并讨论了其对黑洞信息悖论的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用规范/引力对偶中的库仑支特征来构造一个S矩阵，从而研究黑洞的形成与蒸发过程，并探讨黑洞信息悖论。

Method: 通过将初始态构造为在库仑支原点汇聚的膜壳，在引力对偶中其源起具有带帽喉部的几何结构；当帽部红移达到使膜间弦激发不再被抑制时形成俘获面，对应规范理论退禁闭，中间态为长寿命黑洞。

Result: 成功构建了一个描述黑洞形成与蒸发的S矩阵，并对比了体有效场论与对偶规范理论对该过程的描述。

Conclusion: 该构造为理解黑洞信息悖论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: A variety of examples of gauge/gravity duality have a Coulomb branch for the gauge theory dynamics. We exploit this feature to construct an S-matrix, in which the initial state is a shell of branes converging on the origin of the Coulomb branch. In the gravitational dual, the shell of branes sources a geometry with a capped throat; the cap descends from the asymptotic region to larger and larger redshift. A trapped surface forms when the redshift of the cap reaches the point where the excitation of strings stretching between the branes is unsuppressed, and the dual gauge theory deconfines. The intermediate state is a long-lived black hole, which then decays via the slow emission of branes back onto the Coulomb branch. We compare and contrast the descriptions of this process in the bulk effective field theory and the dual gauge theory; and discuss the consequences of this construction for the black hole information paradox.

</details>


### [107] [Asymptotic behaviour of the derivative expansion in the ERG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18397)
*Tim R. Morris*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了精确（泛函）重整化群的导数展开在任何维度下都是发散级数，无论使用指数截断还是更一般的光滑截断。通过无质量λφ⁴微扰理论证明，这种发散首先出现在两圈图。通过理论分析和无穷类两圈、三圈贡献的考察，得出结论：导数展开是一个渐近级数，在发散行为主导之前会先向精确结果收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究精确重整化群导数展开的收敛性质，检验该展开是否在所有维度下都是发散的，并理解其作为渐近级数的行为特征。

Method: 采用无质量λφ⁴微扰理论进行分析，考察指数截断和一般光滑截断下的两圈图贡献，并通过分析无穷多类两圈和三圈贡献来验证结论。

Result: 导数展开在任何维度下都是发散级数；发散首先出现在两圈图层次；该展开是渐近级数，在发散前会先向精确结果收敛。

Conclusion: 导数展开是渐近级数而非收敛级数，其初始行为趋近精确结果，但最终被发散行为主导。

Abstract: We show that the derivative expansion of the exact (functional) renormalization group is a divergent series in any dimension, both for an exponential cutoff and more general smooth cutoffs. We prove this by showing that within massless $λ\varphi^4$ perturbation theory, such divergences arise first at two loops. From several lines of theoretical argument and by analysing infinite classes of two- and three-loop contributions, we conclude that the derivative expansion is an asymptotic series that initially converges towards the exact result before divergent behaviour takes over.

</details>


### [108] [The fermion sector of the SMEFT from asymptotically safe gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18398)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Moritz Gessner,Shouryya Ray*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究量子引力如何通过重整化产生的有效相互作用影响标准模型场，具体分析了渐近安全标准模型中四费米子算符在引力涨落下的重整化群流，发现大多数维度-6 SMEFT算符是不相关的，从而预测了红外SMEFT系数的无量纲比值，同时基于对称性识别了一类可能变得相关的算符子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从渐近安全的量子引力理论出发，预测SMEFT系数值是重要步骤。作者旨在通过研究四费米子算符的量子引力流，确定哪些维度-6算符可以被预测，哪些是自由参数，从而缩小SMEFT参数空间。

Method: 采用功能重整化群方法，计算引力涨落对一组代表性四费米子算符（维度-6）的流方程，分析这些算符在引力耦合下的相关性与无关性，并通过对称性对算符进行分类。

Result: 发现大多数维度-6四费米子算符在渐近安全引力下是不相关的（irrelevant），因此可预测其IR值的特定无量纲比值；同时识别出一类基于对称性可能变得相关（relevant）的算符子集。

Conclusion: 维度-6 SMEFT算符可分为两类：第一类预测为非零但被普朗克尺度压低；第二类在小引力耦合时预测为零，在较大引力耦合时可能成为理论自由参数。这进一步支持了渐近安全的近微扰性质。

Abstract: Quantum gravity impacts Standard Model fields through effective interactions generated by renormalization. These appear as Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) operators, where the effects of new physics are parametrized by the values of the higher-order SMEFT coefficients. As a step towards predicting these SMEFT coefficients from the asymptotically safe Standard Model with quantum gravity, we investigate the flow of a representative set of four-fermion operators under gravitational fluctuations. We strengthen the evidence for the near-perturbative nature of asymptotic safety by finding that these dimension-six-interactions remain irrelevant, resulting in the prediction of specific values for the dimensionless ratios of SMEFT coefficients in the IR. We also find a mechanism that can make a specific subset of these operators relevant. This subset is determined by symmetry considerations. On this basis, we provide a categorization of dimension-six-SMEFT interactions into two categories. Interactions in the first category are predicted to be non-zero, but Planck-scale suppressed. Interactions in the second category are predicted to be zero at small gravitational coupling, but may become free parameters of the theory at larger gravitational couplings.

</details>


### [109] [Warped Numerical Calabi-Yau Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18402)
*Severin Lüst,Fabian Ruehle,Simon Schreyer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在Calabi-Yau三维流形上数值计算GKP框架下的翘曲IIB型弦论通量背景，包括Ricci平坦度量、调和(2,1)-形式和翘曲因子，并在Dwork族五元曲线上验证了两个通量真空（一个靠近conifold点，一个远离之）。在靠近conifold的基准点上，发现约0.5%的CY体积位于喉部区域，翘曲因子比体区大一个数量级。此外提出了改进的采样算法、特征工程谱网络、多步物理信息训练和加权Huber损失等数值技术改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GKP框架下数值计算翘曲通量背景，以便研究弦论紧化中的翘曲效应，特别是靠近conifold奇点附近的物理行为。

Method: 使用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）逼近三个关键成分：Ricci平坦Calabi-Yau度量、表示虚自对偶三形式通量的调和(2,1)-形式、以及求解带源Poisson方程的翘曲因子。在Dwork族五元曲线上实现，并提出了改进的采样算法、特征工程谱网络、多步训练和加权Huber损失等数值技术。

Result: 在靠近conifold的基准点上，约0.5%的CY体积位于喉部区域，翘曲因子比体区大一个数量级。远离conifold的真空作为数值交叉验证。

Conclusion: 成功构建了GKP翘曲背景的数值求解流程，并揭示了靠近conifold奇点时的翘曲喉部几何特征。文中提出的数值技术改进对更广泛的CY度量数值研究具有独立参考价值。

Abstract: We compute numerical warped Type IIB flux backgrounds on Calabi-Yau threefolds following the construction of Giddings, Kachru, and Polchinski. Using physics-informed neural networks, we approximate all three ingredients required by the GKP setup: the Ricci-flat Calabi-Yau metric, the harmonic (2,1)-forms representing the imaginary self-dual three-form flux, and the warp factor, which solves a sourced Poisson equation on the internal manifold. We apply our pipeline to the Dwork family of quintics for two different flux vacua, one near a conifold point and one away from it, the latter serving as a numerical cross-check. With these tools, we study the singular bulk problem, and find that for our benchmark point close to the conifold, approximately 0.5 percent of the total Calabi-Yau volume sits in the throat, and the warp factor is an order of magnitude larger as compared to the bulk. We also introduce several improvements to techniques used for numerical studies of CY metrics and quantities derived from them that might be of interest independently of our application. These include an improved point sampling algorithm that produces samples that are more uniform under the Calabi-Yau measure, a feature-engineered spectral network for the metric, multi-step physics-informed training, and a weighted Huber loss tailored to stiff PDEs with highly non-uniform sources.

</details>


### [110] [(De)Coherence of a quantum system in an anti-de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18407)
*Mandas Biswas,Anupam Mazumdar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在反德西特时空中研究量子谐振子的相干/退相干，通过弯曲时空边界条件量子化引力子，在量子场论框架下得到主方程，并利用半马尔可夫分析计算Lindbladian方程，估计了退相干率和选择定则，发现两种贡献：局域物质-引力子相互作用和AdS全局曲率效应，后者在特定频率和曲率条件下可导致系统重相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AdS时空中量子系统的退相干机制，特别是考虑弯曲时空边界条件对量子相干性的影响，以及全局曲率如何与局域引力相互作用竞争，探讨重相干的可能性。

Method: 采用弯曲时空量子场论框架，对满足非平凡边界条件的引力子进行量子化，在G和1/ω²的首阶下将引力场迹掉得到主方程；运用半马尔可夫分析推导Lindbladian方程，计算退相干率和选择定则。

Result: 退相干率包含两类贡献：一类来自物质与引力子的局域相互作用，另一类来自AdS全局曲率。当谐振子频率与全局曲率匹配时，曲率项可导致系统重相干。在适当近似下可恢复平直时空极限。

Conclusion: AdS时空的全局曲率对量子相干性有非平凡影响，不仅导致退相干，还能在特定条件下诱导重相干现象，揭示了弯曲时空量子效应与引力量子化的深层联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the coherence/decoherence of a quantum harmonic oscillator in anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime by quantising the graviton in a curved background with a nontrivial boundary condition. In the quantum-field-theory framework, we obtain a master equation by tracing away the gravitational field at the leading order in G and 1/omega^2, where omega is related to the trapped frequency of the harmonic oscillator. We will proceed with a semi-Markovian analysis to compute the Lindbladian equation and estimate the decoherence rate and selection rules at the leading order, which come in two kinds: one responsible for the local interaction between matter and graviton, and the other related to the AdS global curvature. The latter determines the recoherence of the quantum system for a certain choice of the trapped harmonic oscillator's frequency and the global curvature. We also demonstrate that we recover the flat spacetime limit by taking appropriate approximations.

</details>


### [111] [Refined Vafa-Witten invariants for toric surfaces from supersymmetric localization in 5D gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18410)
*Osama Khlaif,Boris Pioline,Alessandro Tanzini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ $U(N)$ SYM 理论在环面Kähler曲面 $S$ 乘以圆环 $\mathbb{S}^1_{\boldsymbol\beta}$ 上的配分函数，通过局域化方法将其化为Cartan环面上的积分，并发现对于 $U(2)$ 规范群，贡献极点与半稳定无挠层模空间中的环面不动点一一对应，且非偶第一Chern类时贡献独立于等变参数并等于该模空间的 $\chi_{y^2}$-亏格，从而与 refined Vafa-Witten 不变量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前在 $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ SYM 理论在 $S$ 上以及纯 $\mathcal{N}=1$ SYM 在 $S\times \mathbb{S}^1_{\boldsymbol\beta}$ 上的工作扩展到具有伴随超多重态的 $\mathcal{N}=1$ SYM 理论，以理解其配分函数与模空间不变量之间的关系。

Method: 采用路径积分局域化方法，将配分函数简化为沿着Cartan环面的积分，积分核为每个仿射片上的Nekrasov 5D配分函数的乘积。对于 $U(2)$ 规范群，识别并处理积分核的无穷多极点（阶数不超过 $\chi(S)-2$），并通过自然围道积分方案提取贡献。

Result: 贡献极点与半稳定无挠层模空间中的环面不动点一一对应。对于非偶第一Chern类，极点贡献独立于等变参数 $\varepsilon_1,\varepsilon_2$，求和等于该模空间的 $\chi_{y^2}$-亏格（其中 $y^2=e^{-\boldsymbol\beta m_{\rm adj}}$），因此与 refined Vafa-Witten 不变量一致。对于偶Chern类，配分函数依赖于 $\varepsilon_1,\varepsilon_2$ 和 $y$，与有理 refined Vafa-Witten 不变量的关系尚不明确。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ SYM 配分函数与模空间不变量之间的深刻联系，但偶Chern类情形仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the partition function of five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ $U(N)$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills (SYM) theory with an adjoint hypermultiplet of mass $m_{\rm adj}$ on a toric Kähler surface $S$ times a circle of radius $\boldsymbolβ$. Extending earlier work in $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ SYM theory on $S$, and in pure $\mathcal{N}=1$ SYM on $S\times \mathbb{S}^1_{\boldsymbolβ}$, we find that the path integral localizes to an integral along the Cartan torus of the product of Nekrasov 5D partition functions for each affine patch. Restricting to the gauge group $U(2)$ for simplicity, the integrand has an infinite set of poles of degree at most $χ(S)-2$. With a natural prescription for integrating around such poles, we find that the contributing poles are in one-to-one correspondence with the torus-fixed points in the moduli space of semi-stable torsion-free sheaves on $S$. Moreover, for non-even first Chern class, their contributions are independent of the equivariant parameters $ε_1,ε_2$ and add up to the $χ_{y^2}$-genus of that moduli space, where $y^2=e^{-\boldsymbolβ m_{\rm adj}}$, and hence coincide with the refined Vafa-Witten invariants. For even Chern class, the partition function depends on the equivariant parameters $ε_1,ε_2$ as well as $y$, and its relation to rational, refined Vafa-Witten invariants remains unclear.

</details>


### [112] [Global Asymptotics, the Swampland Conjectures, and Preheating of String Moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18442)
*Leia Price,Kuver Sinha,Robert Wiley Deal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Swampland猜想对自共振预热过程的影响，指出局部势能曲率（特别是de Sitter猜想的 tachyonic 分支）和大场渐近行为共同决定了模场的共振效率，同时Swampland距离猜想预测的轻态塔作为随机环境会重塑而非打开新的共振通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland猜想的宇宙学含义通常聚焦于暴胀模型构建，但其对暴胀后或弦模场位移后的剧烈非绝热动力学（如预热过程）的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 通过自共振预热的视角，分析势能局部曲率（refined de Sitter猜想的 tachyonic 分支）和渐近行为对共振效率的影响；在LVS、KKLT紧致化、膨胀子势及α吸引子模型中研究 tachyonic 共振；将Swampland距离猜想的轻态塔建模为有效随机环境，研究其对预热的修正。

Result: 发现局部曲率信息不足以确定共振效率，势能的大场渐近行为（平台、势垒、逃逸）决定了模场如何采样不稳定区域；轻态主要通过对共振带的涂抹、偏移和轻微不稳定诱导来重塑共振结构，而非打开全新的再加热通道。

Conclusion: Swampland物理通过控制势能的确定性曲率结构以及来自涌现轻态的随机修正来影响预热过程，两种结构共同作用而非单一因素主导。

Abstract: While the cosmological implications of the Swampland Conjectures are usually discussed in the context of inflationary model building, they also have implications for the violent, non-adiabatic dynamics that can follow inflation or any displacement of string moduli. We study this question through the lens of self-resonant preheating, where we point out that two Swampland motivated structures play central roles. The first is the local curvature of the potential: the tachyonic branch of the refined de Sitter Conjecture singles out the kind of negative curvature that can drive tachyonic amplification. We show, however, that local curvature data is not enough; the large field asymptotics of the potential determines how the modulus samples the unstable region. Thus, plateaus, barriers, and runaways can lead to different resonance efficiencies; we study tachyonic resonance for bulk moduli in LVS and KKLT compactifications, as well as for typical blow-up moduli potentials and alpha-attractor models. The second Swampland motivated structure pertains to the tower of light states predicted by the Swampland Distance Conjecture. Modeling a finite subset of such states as an effective stochastic environment within which a string modulus preheats, we find that the light states mainly reshape existing resonance bands by smearing, shifting, and mildly seeding instabilities, rather than opening a robust new reheating channel. Our results suggest that Swampland physics affects preheating by controlling both the deterministic curvature structure of the potential as well as stochastic corrections from emergent light states.

</details>


### [113] [Viscoelastic and thermal response on nuclear pasta states at finite baryon density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18463)
*Nicolás Grandi,Scarlett Rebolledo,Aldo Vera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Skyrme模型的解析解描述非均匀强子凝聚体（重子管/层晶体）的基态，通过格林-库柏公式计算其粘弹性和热响应，并与核意面相结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非均匀强子凝聚体（模拟核意面相）的粘弹性和热输运性质，为致密天体（如中子星壳层）的动力学和热演化提供微观理论基础。

Method: 采用Skyrme模型在3+1维中的解析解描述重子管和重子层晶体结构；在低温下将最低能激发简化为1+1维自由无质量标量场论；应用格林-库柏公式计算弹性张量和响应系数。

Result: 得到了粘弹性系数和热响应系数的解析表达式，并与核意面相的已知结果进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: Skyrme模型框架能有效描述非均匀强子凝聚体的低能响应，理论结果与核意面相现象在定性乃至定量上吻合，为理解致密物质输运性质提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We compute the viscoelastic and thermal response of non-homogeneous hadronic condensates at finite baryon density representing crystals of baryonic tubes and layers. We describe them using analytic solutions of the Skyrme model in $3+1$ dimensions as ground states for a perturbative approach. At low enough temperatures, the lowest-energy excitations are described by a free massless scalar field theory in $1+1$ dimensions. We apply the Green-Kubo formulas to such excitations to obtain the elasticity tensor and other response coefficients. The analytic results of our computations are compared with available results on the nuclear pasta phase.

</details>


### [114] [Gravitational Memory Beyond Null Infinity through Finite-Distance Carrollian Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18675)
*Felipe Diaz,Sercan Hüsnügil,Oriana Labrin,Leonardo Sanhueza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限距离零超曲面上的引力记忆效应，通过Carrollian几何框架将Bondi位移记忆推广为更广泛的准局域Carrollian响应，并在Robinson-Trautman时空中精确验证了该框架，揭示了渐近记忆是准局域记忆在大半径极限下的投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的引力记忆效应研究局限于零无穷远，而真实的引力波探测器位于有限距离。本文旨在建立有限距离零超曲面上的引力记忆理论框架，将Bondi记忆推广到有限距离并揭示其与渐近记忆的关系。

Method: 采用Carrollian几何描述有限距离零超曲面，利用Robinson-Trautman时空作为精确可解辐射模型。通过Bondi规范变换提取渐近数据，在微扰框架下分析有限屏幕记忆与Bondi位移记忆的关系，并考虑大半径极限和晚期屏幕趋近视界的行为。

Result: 有限屏幕记忆的主迹零部在大半径极限下约化为Bondi位移记忆，但有限距离修正保留了额外聚焦、嵌入依赖、角漂移、Coulomb数据和近区信息。趋近Schwarzschild视界的晚期屏幕仅保留各向同性零Brown-York应力。同时构造了含宇宙学常数的Robinson-Trautman解至二阶辐射振幅。

Conclusion: Bondi记忆是更广泛的准局域Carrollian响应在渐近区域上的普适投影。有限距离引力记忆包含了更丰富的物理内容，为未来引力波观测提供了超越渐近近似的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational memory beyond null infinity by studying finite-distance null hypersurfaces endowed with Carrollian geometry. We show that the intrinsic degenerate geometry, optical data, and null Brown--York tensor of a finite null screen define a quasilocal Carrollian dissipative system, providing a natural framework to characterize the residual geometric response after the passage of radiation. To make this construction explicit and compare it with the standard asymptotic description, we use Robinson--Trautman spacetimes as an exactly solvable radiative setting. For asymptotically flat Robinson--Trautman geometries, we transform the solution to Bondi gauge and extract the asymptotic data directly in terms of the Robinson--Trautman field. In the linearized sector, the Bondi shear is purely electric and produces the standard displacement-memory effect associated with the relaxation toward the final Schwarzschild geometry. We show that the leading large-radius tracefree component of the finite-screen memory reduces to the Bondi displacement memory, while finite-distance corrections retain additional focusing, embedding dependence, angular drift, Coulombic data, and near-zone information. Thus, Bondi memory emerges as the universal asymptotic projection of a broader quasilocal Carrollian response. Late-time screens approaching the final Schwarzschild horizon exhibit exponentially decaying non-isotropic Carrollian data, leaving only the isotropic null Brown--York stress. As a by-product, we construct the Robinson--Trautman solution with nonzero cosmological constant to second order in the radiative amplitude and use the holographic dictionary to study the associated energy fluxes and find that the resulting charge does not obey a universal monotonicity property.

</details>


### [115] [3d SUSY enhancement with non-trivial Coulomb branch via 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ SCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19021)
*Ryo Hamachika,Takahiro Nishinaka,Shou Tanigawa,Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了一个流到(A₂,D₄) Argyres-Douglas理论的R-扭约化约化的3d N=2 CS物质理论，得到了一个具有非平凡Coulomb分支（轨道空间ℂ²/ℤ₂）和平凡Higgs分支的3d N=4 SCFT，并识别了4d U(1) R-荷与3d R-荷及涌现Coulomb分支味荷的混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前所有从4d N=2 SCFT的R-扭约化得到的3d N=4 SCFT都有着平凡的Coulomb分支，本文旨在构建第一个具有非平凡Coulomb分支的例子。

Method: 构造了一个特定的3d N=2 Chern-Simons物质理论，使其在红外流到(A₂,D₄) Argyres-Douglas理论的R-扭约化。

Result: 得到的3d N=4 SCFT具有非平凡的Coulomb分支ℂ²/ℤ₂，而Higgs分支平凡；4d U(1) R-荷是3d R-荷与一个无4d起源的涌现Coulomb分支味荷的混合。

Conclusion: 这是该类中第一个具有非平凡Coulomb分支的例子，揭示了R-扭约化下新的现象。

Abstract: In recent years, a variety of three-dimensional (3d) $\mathcal{N}=2$ Chern--Simons (CS) matter theories have been constructed that flow to 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ superconformal field theories (SCFTs) obtained from $R$-twisted reductions of four-dimensional (4d) $\mathcal{N}=2$ SCFTs. In all previously studied examples, the resulting 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ SCFTs have trivial Coulomb branches. In this paper, we construct a 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ CS matter theory that flows to the $R$-twisted reduction of the $(A_2,D_4)$ Argyres--Douglas theory. This gives the first example in this class whose infrared 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ SCFT has a non-trivial Coulomb branch, which we argue is given by the orbifold $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$, while its Higgs branch is trivial. We further identify the 4d $U(1)$ R-charge as a mixing of a 3d R-charge with an emergent 3d Coulomb branch flavor charge that has no 4d origin.

</details>


### [116] [The complete massless singlet spectrum in the free-field construction of heterotic strings on Calabi--Yau orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19289)
*Vladimir Belavin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用自由场构造计算了紧致化在Berglund-Hübsch Calabi-Yau轨形上的杂化弦的无质量E6单态全谱，通过结合N=2最小模型表示的Shapovalov矩阵秩与轨形扭转型扇区结构，成功再现了已知的五次轨形谱(17,21)及234个单态，修正了五次形本身的单态数为330而非此前认为的326，并计算了两个新轨形的谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了精确计算杂化弦在Calabi-Yau轨形上紧致化的无质量E6单态谱，特别是解决Gepner点上五次形单态数(330 vs 326)的争议问题，并验证镜像对称性。

Method: 采用自由场构造方法，将N=2最小模型不可约表示的Shapovalov矩阵秩编码的精确内容与轨形的扭转型扇区结构相结合，构建一般顶点并计算无质量E6单态谱。

Result: 成功再现五次轨形(17,21)谱及234个单态；修正五次形单态数为330(而非326)，与Kachru-Witten的Landau-Ginzburg计算一致；计算了两个新轨形Z5[0,1,2,3,4] (21,1,210)和Z5[0,0,0,1,4] (49,5,258)的谱；所有四个例子均满足精确镜像对称性检验。

Conclusion: 自由场构造方法能正确计算杂化弦在Berglund-Hübsch CY轨形上的无质量谱，修正了此前五次形单态数的几何描述值，且所有结果均通过镜像对称性验证。

Abstract: We apply the free-field construction of the heterotic string compactified on Berglund--Hübsch Calabi--Yau orbifolds by computing the full spectrum of massless $E_6$ singlets. The contribution of the descendant vertices is obtained by combining the exact content of the irreducible $N=2$ minimal model representations, encoded in the ranks of the Shapovalov matrices, with the twisted sector structure of the orbifold. The method reproduces the known spectrum of the quintic orbifold with Hodge numbers $(17,21)$, namely $17$ generations, $21$ antigenerations and $234$ singlets. For the quintic itself we obtain $330$ singlets. We show that this number, rather than the frequently quoted value $326$, obtained from the geometric description, is the correct one at the Gepner point, in agreement with the Landau--Ginzburg computation of Kachru and Witten. We also provide an explicit construction of the general vertices. We then compute the new spectra of the two remaining quintic orbifolds, $Z_5[0,1,2,3,4]$ with $(21,1,210)$ and $Z_5[0,0,0,1,4]$ with $(49,5,258)$, where the exceptional Hodge number $h^{2,1}=49$ arises from the twisted sectors. All four examples satisfy exact mirror-symmetry checks.

</details>


### [117] [Pure D-brane Black Holes: BPS Counting and non-BPS Vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07927)
*Abhishek Chowdhury,Sourav Maji*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的计算框架，利用计算代数几何和数值拓扑技术分析IIA型弦理论中4荷极值黑洞的微观真空结构。对超对称D2-D2-D2-D6构型计算了第14阶螺旋度迹指标，通过参数单值方法重现了U对偶预言的简并度；对非BPS极值纯D膜系统（用反D6膜替换D6膜并加入不相容R对称旋转），利用Gröbner基证明零能经典基态缺失，并通过Morse-Bott变形和门控软捕获拓扑正则化方法，刻画了经典能量景观中的非紧致Coulomb支、边际稳定子流形和双简并低能稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对4荷极值黑洞微观真空结构的分析多局限于特定超对称构型或有限荷空间，缺乏统一的计算框架来处理超对称与非超对称两种情形。本文旨在填补这一空白，将计算代数几何与数值拓扑方法系统应用于D膜有效量子力学，以统一方式解析超对称和非BPS两类构型的真空结构。

Method: 1) 超对称扇区：采用参数单值方法（parametric monodromy method）解析(1,1,1,5)和(1,1,1,6)构型的真空，计算第14阶螺旋度迹指标；2) 非BPS扇区：用反D6膜替换D6膜并施加不相容R对称旋转以显式破缺超对称，利用解析Gröbner基分析标量势，采用Morse-Bott变形和门控软捕获作为拓扑正则化方法处理连续平坦方向。

Result: 1) 超对称情形下，成功重现了U对偶图像预言的简并度；2) 非BPS情形下，证明了零能经典基态缺失，识别出非紧致Coulomb支、边际稳定子流形以及一组孤立的双简并低能稳态。

Conclusion: 本文建立的计算框架能够统一处理超对称和非超对称两类4荷极值纯D膜系统，展现了计算代数几何和数值拓扑方法在弦理论真空结构分析中的强大适用性，为更广泛的D膜构型研究提供了可推广的技术路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a unified computational framework to analyze the microscopic vacuum structure of 4-charge extremal black holes in Type IIA string theory, applying techniques from computational algebraic geometry and numerical topology to their pure D-brane effective quantum mechanics. We apply this approach to two physically distinct configurations. First, in the supersymmetric sector, we compute the $14^{\text{th}}$ helicity trace index of $\frac{1}{8}$-BPS, $N=8$, D2-D2-D2-D6 configurations dual to D1-D5-P-KK monopole dyonic black holes. Extending previous work to higher charges, we employ a parametric monodromy method to explicitly resolve the vacua for the $(1,1,1,5)$ and $(1,1,1,6)$ configurations, reproducing the degeneracies predicted by the U-dual picture. Second, we apply complementary techniques to a configuration where supersymmetry is explicitly broken at the level of the effective action. The corresponding 4-charge non-BPS extremal pure D-brane system is obtained by replacing the D6-brane with an anti-D6-brane and assigning incompatible R-symmetry rotations to different brane triplets. Analyzing the associated scalar potential using analytical Gröbner bases, we demonstrate the absence of zero-energy classical ground states. To handle the continuous flat directions populating the non-BPS landscape, we implement specific topological regularizations, namely Morse-Bott deformations and gated soft-trapping. These methods allow us to systematically characterize the classical energy landscape, identifying a non-compact Coulomb branch, marginally bound stabilizer submanifolds, and an isolated collection of doubly degenerate low-energy stable states.

</details>


### [118] [Polynomial Initial-State Jumps and Christoffel Transforms in Krylov Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05294)
*Abhishek Chowdhury,Ajit Prasad Mahapatra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了态复杂度（State Krylov/spread complexity）在固定哈密顿量$H$下随初始态$|K_0\rangle$变化的重整化结构，建立了Christoffel重加权、正交多项式矩递推、Christoffel-Darboux核投影与复杂度之间的统一数学框架，并在Heisenberg-Weyl/Charlier振子、SU(2)/Krawtchouk链及紧束缚/Chebyshev链等模型中验证了其大指标渐近行为、有限时间复杂性及种子族扩展性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明态复杂度在固定$H$下对初始态选择的依赖机制，统一描述Lanczos链在循环子空间中的重组行为，并建立从正交多项式理论到量子动力学的完整对应关系。

Method: 通过Christoffel正重加权将初始态变化映射为参考测度乘以$|Q|^2$的修饰；利用有限带转移将Fourier正交多项式矩映射为移位振幅；通过Christoffel-Darboux核的有限秩投影计算累积概率与复杂度。在具体模型中引入无根余项递推、乘积恒等式与乘积Gram分解等工具处理终端商结构与Weel对偶。

Result: 证明了有限时间内每个固定跳变的复杂度有限性；给出了Charlier-Hermite标度下连续谱支撑的过渡；揭示了Weyl反射对相反自旋权重的配对作用；建立了种子族矩阵值母测度与多项式算子后裔的相对演算扩展。

Conclusion: 求解的循环问题可确定一族固定$H$动力学，将制备依赖性从生成元或Hilbert空间维度的变化中分离出来，为量子复杂度理论提供了统一的数学基础。

Abstract: State Krylov, or spread, complexity belongs to the cyclic pair $(H,|K_0\rangle)$, so changing the initial state at fixed-$H$ reorganizes the Lanczos chain within the reference cyclic subspace. For normalized polynomial descendants $|ψ_Q\rangle \propto Q(H)|K_0\rangle$, this reorganization is the positive Christoffel reweighting of the reference measure by $|Q|^2$. Orthogonality gives a finite-band transfer from reference Fourier-orthogonal-polynomial moments to shifted amplitudes, while a finite-rank projection of its Christoffel-Darboux kernel yields cumulative probabilities and spread complexity. Complex superpositions, confluent roots, deletion of spectral atoms and terminal closure enter the same construction. In the Heisenberg-Weyl/Charlier oscillator, root-free remainder recurrences govern arbitrary number-state jumps. Their large-index behavior distinguishes generic shifts from resonant deletion of Poisson atoms, determines the Jacobi asymptotics, and proves the finiteness of the complexity at finite time for every fixed jump. In finite $SU(2)$/Krawtchouk and tight-binding/Chebyshev chains, product identities and product-Gram factorizations in the terminal quotient determine all weight-state and localized-site connectors through the terminal edge, while Weyl reflection pairs opposite spin weights. The first-jump Charlier-Hermite scaling carries it to continuous spectral support. Finite seed families admit a matrix-valued parent measure, and the relative calculus extends to polynomial operator descendants whenever the Liouvillian has a self-adjoint realization for the chosen inner product. A solved cyclic problem determines a family of fixed-$H$ dynamics and separates preparation dependence from changes of the generator or Hilbert-space dimension.

</details>

### [119] [Integrable multi-species SSEP with reactive particle species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18959)
*Mathieu Dabrowski,Loïc Poulain d'Andecy,Eric Ragoucy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Yang-Baxter方程集合论解构造的可积排斥马尔可夫过程，推广了多物种SSEP。首先引入(p,1)-SSEP，其包含可蒸发/凝聚/转化的"反应性"粒子物种，完成了周期情形下扇区的组合研究，并利用反射方程解的Baxter化证明了周期和两种开边界情形下的可积性。进而推广到(p,q)-SSEP（任意数量反应性物种），证明周期情形的可积性并引入广义可积边界。最后定义了相关物理量，并在一种可积边界下计算了开放(p,q)-SSEP非平衡稳态的物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可积排斥过程（如多物种SSEP）无法处理粒子种类间存在转化、蒸发/凝聚等反应性动力学的场景，亟需构建更一般的可积模型以描述此类非平衡统计物理问题。

Method: 基于Yang-Baxter方程的集合论解构建转移矩阵；利用反射方程解的Baxter化构造开边界；通过组合分析研究扇区结构；借助可积性（RTT关系）证明模型的可积性。

Result: (1) 完成了(p,1)-SSEP周期扇区的完整组合分类；(2) 证明了(p,1)-SSEP在周期和两类开边界下的可积性；(3) 证明了(p,q)-SSEP在周期情形下的可积性并引入广义可积边界；(4) 在一种可积边界下解析计算了开放(p,q)-SSEP非平衡稳态的物理量。

Conclusion: 成功构造并严格证明了一类包含反应性粒子物种的可积排斥过程，为研究具有粒子转化/蒸发/凝聚等反应动力学的非平衡系统提供了精确可解模型框架。

Abstract: We investigate integrable exclusion Markov processes constructed from set-theoretical solutions of the Yang-Baxter Equation (YBE) that generalise the multi-species Symmetric Simple Exclusion Process (SSEP). We first introduce the process called the $ (p,1) $-SSEP that is analogous to the multi-species SSEP but with an extra particle species qualified as reactive. Reactive species are able to evaporate and condensate by pairs or to transform by pairs depending on the interpretation. We provide a full combinatorial study of the sectors in the periodic case. We prove the integrability of the process in the periodic case and in the open case for two types of boundaries that we introduce using Baxterisations of solutions of the reflection equation. Next we move on to the process called $ (p,q) $-SSEP, i.e. the multi-species SSEP with an arbitrary number of reactive particle species. We prove its integrability in the periodic case and, for the open case, we introduce integrable boundaries generalising the ones considered for the $ (p,1) $-SSEP. Finally, we define physical quantities relevant to the models and we compute them in the non-equilibrium stationary state of the open $ (p,q) $-SSEP for one type of integrable boundaries.

</details>


### [120] [Vakonomic Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18312)
*Ritoban Roy-Chowdhury,Mohammad Sina Nabizadeh,Oliver Gross,Anthony Gruber,Albert Chern*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于保体积微分同胚群上测地线方程的可压缩欧拉方程新型离散化方法，采用vakonomic变分视角（而非Lagrange-d'Alembert视角）处理非完整约束，使得离散流体轨迹保持(sub-)Riemannian流形上的测地线性质，获得Lie-Poisson结构、离散重标对称性、Casimir不变量机器精度保持以及离散Kelvin环流定理，并利用低秩Clebsch变量动量映射表示实现低分辨率下的稳定鲁棒长期模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Koopman表示离散化中非完整约束的变分处理缺乏共识的问题，提出采用vakonomic视角替代Lagrange-d'Alembert视角，以在离散层面保持流体的几何结构和守恒律。

Method: 将欧拉方程视为保体积微分同胚群上的测地线方程，通过离散Koopman表示编码微分同胚及其无穷小生成元，引入非完整约束，采用vakonomic变分原理（而非Lagrange-d'Alembert原理）推导离散动力学，得到(sub-)Riemannian流形上的测地线轨迹，并利用低秩Clebsch变量高效表示动量映射。

Result: 所得vakonomic动力学具有Lie-Poisson结构，离散解保持重标对称性，Casimir不变量达到机器精度，满足离散Kelvin环流定理；即使低网格分辨率下，该方法也能实现稳定一致的长期模拟，增强鲁棒性和物理真实性。

Conclusion: vakonomic变分视角为不可压缩欧拉方程的几何离散化提供了更优的框架，在保持物理对称性和守恒律的同时提升了数值稳定性。

Abstract: We introduce a novel discretization of the incompressible Euler equations based on their interpretation as geodesic equations on the Lie group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms. It is well known that encoding diffeomorphisms and their infinitesimal generators through a discretized Koopman representation places a nonholonomic constraint on discrete velocities, for which there is no consensus on a variational treatment. We show that taking the vakonomic perspective, as opposed to the usual perspective of Lagrange--d'Alembert, yields discrete fluid trajectories that remain geodesics on a (sub-)Riemannian manifold. In particular, the resulting vakonomic dynamics are Lie--Poisson and their solutions admit a discrete relabeling symmetry, leading to machine-precision satisfaction of Casimir invariants along with a discrete analogue of Kelvin's Circulation Theorem. Using an efficient momentum map representation based on low-rank Clebsch variables, we show that these vakonomic fluids behave stably and consistently even at low grid resolutions, leading to increased robustness and physical realism in the long term.

</details>


### [121] [Topological Foundations of Multi-Field Instabilities in Continua: Part 1: Foundations Part 2:Analytical Formulation for 1-D Spin Chains Part 3: Numerical Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18995)
*Klaus Regenauer-Lieb,Francois Nicot,Amir Saker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个无参数、基于拓扑的多场失稳分类框架，将Maxwell刚性计数扩展到动态非平衡过程。通过离散VMC接触公式、奇偶定理和Gateway数概念，揭示了奇数通道的拓扑零模和陀螺泵浦放大机制。系列包括一维自旋链的解析解和数值验证，并提出了可实验验证的固结仪协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多场失稳理论缺乏统一的拓扑分类框架，传统Maxwell刚性计数仅限于静态平衡系统，无法描述颗粒连续介质中非平衡、动态的不稳定性。本文旨在建立一个无参数、拓扑驱动的理论，将接触尺度拓扑与宏观多物理场耦合联系起来，揭示由拓扑奇偶性决定的基本失稳机制。

Method: 本文采用三部分结构：(1) 建立离散VMC接触公式，推导奇偶定理，定义Gateway数；(2) 对一维自旋链进行解析求解；(3) 使用四精度数值积分对斜对称Onsager链进行验证，并与DEM观测关联。

Result: 奇偶定理表明奇数通道N必存在结构零模（Gateway层），打破了时间反演对称性。当Gateway数≥1时，产生沿零模方向的非模态瞬态放大。一维分析表明N=3产生长期漂移，N=4产生谐波约束。数值验证了奇偶性对比，偶数链频率~γπ/N。VMC通道可与DEM可观测量映射。

Conclusion: 本文建立的拓扑分类框架成功将Maxwell刚性计数扩展到动态非平衡过程，揭示了由拓扑奇偶性决定的基本失稳模式。该理论无参数、可验证，为理解颗粒连续体的多场失稳提供了新范式。

Abstract: This three-part series establishes a parameter-free, topological classification of multi-field instability in granular continua, extending Maxwell's rigidity count to dynamic, non-equilibrium processes.
  Part 1 (Foundations): a discrete Volumetric-Mechanical-Configurational (VMC) contact formulation maps contact-scale topology to macroscopic multiphysics coupling. A Parity Theorem, $\det(\mathsf{L})=(-1)^N\det(\mathsf{L})$, forces a structural null-mode for every odd channel count $N$, creating "Gateway" layers of broken time-reversal symmetry; once the basis-invariant Gateway number $\mathcal{G}_{\rm inv}\geq 1$, gyroscopic pumping drives non-modal transient amplification along the null direction.
  Part 2 (analytical, 1-D spin chains): the minimal Gateway is the $N=3$ VMC contact, whose skew block $\mathsf{L}\in\mathfrak{so}(3)$ carries a persistent zero eigenvalue and an unresisted configurational drift that operates even without friction. In an acyclic chain (first Betti number $β_1=0$) this isolates dilatancy; closed-form solutions give secular drift for $N=3$ and harmonic confinement for $N=4$.
  Part 3 (numerical upscaling): quad-precision integration of tridiagonal skew-symmetric Onsager chains ($N=3$ to $50$) confirms the contrast between odd-$N$ secular drift and even-$N$ confinement on invariant tori, with even-chain frequencies scaling as $|λ_{\min}^{\rm even}|\simγπ/N$. VMC channels map to measurable DEM observables, enabling parameter-free evaluation of $\mathcal{G}$ and four falsifiable oedometer protocols.

</details>


### [122] [Boundary Free Energies in Disordered Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18326)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无序伊辛模型边界修正对自由能的影响，提出一维反例表明表面自由能密度可能依赖于van Hove序列且随机修正不一定依概率收敛。在Dobrushin唯一性区域内证明了自由-固定边界自由能差在期望、几乎必然和L^p意义下的收敛性。推导了高斯边界耦合的精确有限体积插值恒等式，并分析了缝翻自由能差的方差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无序伊辛模型中边界修正项的性质，特别是表面自由能密度是否与van Hove序列无关、随机修正是否依概率收敛等基本问题，以及低温柔斯-爱德华兹模型的难题。

Method: 通过一维独立同分布反例分析表面自由能密度与序列的相关性；利用Dobrushin唯一性理论证明高维立方体上的收敛性；推导高斯边界耦合的精确有限体积插值恒等式；通过方差不等式分析缝翻自由能差。

Result: 给出了表面自由能密度依赖于van Hove序列且随机修正不依概率收敛的一维反例；在d≥2维Dobrushin唯一性区域内证明了自由-固定边界自由能差的依期望、几乎必然和L^p收敛；得到了缝翻自由能差的方差上界Var(D_L)≤4v|S_L|。

Conclusion: 边界修正的收敛性高度依赖于边界条件、归一化方式和有限体积序列的选择；一维反例表明对称性和有限矩不足以确定刚度指数；低温柔斯-爱德华兹问题仍然开放。

Abstract: The boundary correction to the free energy of a disordered Ising model depends on the boundary condition, the normalization, and the finite-volume sequence. We give a one-dimensional i.i.d. counterexample showing that the surface free-energy density need not be independent of the van Hove sequence and that the corresponding random correction need not converge in probability. For bounded couplings in the Dobrushin uniqueness regime on cubic boxes in dimensions $d\geq2$, we prove convergence of the normalized free-to-fixed boundary free-energy difference in expectation, almost surely, and in every $L^p$, $1\leq p<\infty$. For Gaussian boundary couplings, we derive an exact finite-volume interpolation identity and explain why it does not by itself imply a low-temperature surface limit. For a seam-flip free-energy difference $D_L$ across a set $S_L$ of independent symmetric bonds of variance $v$, we prove $\Var(D_L)\leq4v\abs{S_L}$; one-dimensional examples show that symmetry and finite moments alone do not determine a stiffness exponent, and the low-temperature Gaussian Edwards--Anderson problem remains open.

</details>


### [123] [A converse to generalized Runcorn's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18379)
*Vjekoslav Kovač,Ivica Smolić*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究解决了广义Runcorn定理的反问题，该问题源于月球磁性的明显悖论。具体而言，研究刻画了使得特定积分恒为零的平方可积复函数f的空间，该积分涉及调和函数u和v的梯度内积。解空间依赖于半径a和b（满足r_-<a<b<r_+）被视为可变还是固定。通过将f展开为球谐函数来描述解，并给出了显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的动机源于月球磁性的明显悖论。月球被认为没有全球性的偶极磁场，但月球岩石却记录了古磁场信息。Runcorn定理及其推广为解释这一悖论提供了数学框架，通过研究该反问题，可以更好地理解哪些物质分布函数f能够产生观测到的磁场特征。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）将问题形式化为寻找平方可积复函数f使得特定积分恒为零；2）分别考虑半径a和b可变与固定两种情况；3）将函数f展开为球谐函数进行谱分析；4）通过数学推导给出解的显式表征。

Result: 研究得到了函数f的完整表征：当半径a和b可变时，解空间受到更严格的限制；当半径a和b固定时，解空间更为宽泛。解通过球谐展开的形式给出，并提供了显式表示。具体而言，f必须满足特定的正交条件，这些条件与球谐函数的阶数有关。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义Runcorn定理提供了完整的数学刻画，解决了月球磁性问题中的一个关键反问题。研究结果不仅深化了对行星磁场生成机制的理解，还为解释月球岩石记录古磁场而现今无全球偶极场这一悖论提供了数学依据。

Abstract: We study an inverse problem for generalized Runcorn's theorem motivated by an apparent paradox of lunar magnetism. In mathematical terms, we characterize square-integrable complex functions $f$ such that $$ \int_{\{ x\in\mathbb{R}^n : a \leq |x| \leq b \}} f(x) \nabla u(x)\cdot\nabla v(x)\,\mathrm{d}\mathrm{V}(x) = 0 $$ for every complex harmonic function $u$ in the inner ball $|x|<r_+$ and every complex harmonic function $v$ in the exterior region $|x|>r_-$ that vanishes at infinity. The solution space depends on whether the radii $a$ and $b$ such that $r_-<a<b<r_+$ are regarded as varying or fixed. The solutions are described through the expansion of $f$ into spherical harmonics, and explicit representations are provided.

</details>


### [124] [Tau functions and correlation functions of the bosonic universal character hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18780)
*Denghui Li,Jinzhou Liu,Zhaowen Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了玻色子普适特征（UC）层级结构，基于带电自由玻色子框架研究了其tau函数，证明了tau函数可由有序指数算子表示，并推导了关联函数——该关联函数是广义相位模型关联函数的逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建玻色子UC层级结构，并在带电自由玻色子框架下研究其tau函数和关联函数的表达形式。

Method: 利用带电自由玻色子框架和Lie代数$\mathfrak{\widehat{gl}}_{2\infty}$，通过有序指数算子表示tau函数，并推导关联函数。

Result: 玻色子UC层级的tau函数可由有序指数算子表示，关联函数可表示为UC的乘积，且该关联函数是广义相位模型关联函数的逆。

Conclusion: 成功构建了玻色子UC层级结构，揭示了其tau函数和关联函数的代数结构，并建立了与广义相位模型之间的对偶关系。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the construction of the bosonic universal character (UC) hierarchy, whose tau functions are investigated within the framework of charged free bosons. The Lie algebra corresponding to the bosonic UC hierarchy is $\mathfrak{\widehat{gl}}_{2\infty}$. It is shown that the tau functions of bosonic UC hierarchy can be represented based on a series of ordered exponential operators. Furthermore, we derive correlation functions of the bosonic UC hierarchy, which can be expressed as the product of UCs. It is worth noting that the correlation functions of the bosonic UC hierarchy are the inverse of the correlation functions of the generalized phase model.

</details>


### [125] [Towards General Relativity as a generalized Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18837)
*Hartwig Winterroth,Lorenzo Fatibene*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过推广主丛理论，将广义规范连接应用于经典场论，提出广义Yang-Mills理论，并证明向量丛上的广义主连接等价于含基本焊接形式的仿射连接，从而在适当假设下恢复了真空广义相对论的Vielbein表述，暗示了一种广义规范引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在发展广义规范理论的一个实例，为Yang-Mills理论和广义相对论提供统一语言。

Method: 回顾Lie群纤维丛和广义主丛的基本定义，给出水平提升和局部刻画；在经典场论框架下分析广义主连接的动力学和运动学，提出广义Yang-Mills理论；证明向量丛是广义主丛的特例，且其上的广义主连接等价于含基本焊接形式的仿射连接。

Result: 在适当假设下，利用上述框架恢复了真空广义相对论的Vielbein表述，揭示了广义规范引力理论的可能性。

Conclusion: 广义主丛理论能够统一描述Yang-Mills理论和广义相对论，向量丛上的广义主连接即为仿射连接，为规范引力理论提供了新视角。

Abstract: As an application of the generalized principal bundle theory to covariant Lagrangian field theories, we aim at the development of an instance of generalized gauge theories, with the prospect of a unifying language for Yang-Mills theories and General Relativity. After reviewing the basic definitions of Lie group fiber bundles and generalized principal bundles, we provide horizontal lift and local characterizations of Lie group fiber bundle connections and generalized principal connections. Subsequently, we consider in the framework of classical field theories the kinematics and dynamics sides of generalized principal connections, which lead to a proposed notion of generalized Yang-Mills theories. Finally, we show how vector bundles are examples of generalized principal bundles and that a generalized principal connection on a vector bundle is an affine connection given in terms of basic soldering forms. We are able to recover, under appropriate assumptions, the Vielbein formulation of (vacuum) General Relativity in this setting, hinting at a (generalized) gauge theory of gravity.

</details>


### [126] [Torsionful Killing-Yano Forms under T-Duality: Transformation Conditions and Emergent Isometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18842)
*Özgür Kelekçi,Ümit Ertem,Özgür Açık*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非平凡三形式挠率存在下，阿贝尔T对偶中Killing-Yano p形式的变换法则，推导了与度规无关的坐标自由变换律，并通过多个例子验证，最后给出广义几何与双场论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索存在三形式挠率（NS-NS场）时，Killing-Yano形式在阿贝尔T对偶下的变换行为，填补该领域在含挠率背景下的理论空白。

Method: 使用含挠率的度规相容连接和Buscher T对偶规则，推导坐标自由的Killing-Yano形式变换定律；通过显式匹配假设处理任意阶形式；给出对偶圈分量的标量修正方程。

Result: Killing 1-形式在直接对偶映射下保持的条件是其横向分量与等度规方向无关；对偶圈分量由标量修正方程决定；在Hopf对偶的S^3、Schwarzschild时空和Nappi-Witten平面波等例子中显式验证。

Conclusion: 建立了含挠率背景下Killing-Yano形式的T对偶变换理论，并通过广义几何和双场论框架给出了对偶几何中涌现Killing 1-形式的解释。

Abstract: We investigate the transformation of Killing-Yano (KY) $p$-forms under Abelian T-duality in the presence of a non-trivial three-form torsion. Using a torsionful, metric-compatible connection and the Buscher rules of T-duality, we derive compact, coordinate-free transformation laws applicable to KY forms of arbitrary degree under explicit matching assumptions. In particular, we show that a Killing 1-form is preserved by the direct duality map whenever its transverse components are independent of the isometry direction, with the dual circle component determined by a scalar correction equation. The framework is then applied to several examples, including the Hopf T-dual of $S^3$, Schwarzschild spacetime, and the Nappi-Witten plane wave with exact NS-NS torsion, where we analyze the transformed Killing-Yano forms explicitly. Finally, we give a generalized-geometric and double-field-theoretic interpretation of emergent Killing 1-forms in the T-dual geometry, showing how they arise from generalized Killing data in the original duality frame.

</details>


### [127] [Imaging through rough interfaces: The shower curtain effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18947)
*Cristophe Gomez,Knut Sølna*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的分析方法，将散射层建模为粗糙界面而非随机介质，用于研究"浴帘效应"中图像质量与散射层位置的关系，并推导了图像分辨率和信噪比的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对"浴帘效应"的分析主要将散射层建模为随机介质，但在许多实际物理配置中，散射层更适合描述为粗糙界面。本文旨在弥补这一建模空白。

Method: 将散射层建模为粗糙界面，在此框架下推导图像分辨率和信噪比的显式表征，分析这些量如何依赖于粗糙界面的统计特性及其在物与观察者之间的相对位置。

Result: 获得了图像分辨率和信噪比关于粗糙界面统计特性和相对位置的显式依赖关系。

Conclusion: 基于粗糙界面模型的浴帘效应分析能够更自然地描述许多实际物理配置，为理解散射层位置对成像质量的影响提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The quality of an image observed through a scattering layer, such as a shower curtain, depends strongly on the relative position of the scattering layer between the object and the observer. This well-known phenomenon is commonly referred to as the shower curtain effect. When the scattering layer is placed close to the observer, the image is strongly degraded, whereas if it is located close to the object, the object may still be observed with relatively high resolution. Previous analyses of the shower curtain effect have primarily modeled the scattering layer as a section of a random medium. In this work, we present a new analysis in which the scattering layer is modeled instead as a rough interface, a description that arises naturally in many physical configurations. Within this framework, we derive explicit characterizations of both the image resolution and the signal-to-noise ratio, and determine how these quantities depend on the statistical properties of the rough interface and on its relative location between the object and the observer.

</details>


### [128] [The spectral edge of the quartic SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18998)
*Yukun He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有随机q体相互作用的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型（N个Majorana费米子）。对于q=4的情况，证明了当N通过偶数趋于无穷时，模型的最大特征值几乎必然收敛到一个明确表达式。证明的关键是计算任意固定正温度下的SYK自由能极限，通过引入有限浴插值方法，将四次SYK压力问题归结为局部腔-核恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定SYK模型在q=4时最大特征值的渐近行为，建立其与零温Schwinger-Dyson方程解的联系。

Method: 引入有限浴插值方法（finite-bath interpolation），将四次SYK压力问题转化为局部腔-核恒等式；计算任意固定正温度下的SYK自由能极限；识别零温Schwinger-Dyson压力斜率并将其传递到谱边。

Result: 最大特征值λ₁/√N几乎必然收敛到4∫₀^∞ g₀(t)⁴ dt ≈ 0.32504，其中g₀为零温四次Schwinger-Dyson方程的唯一解。

Conclusion: 成功获得了SYK模型最大特征值的渐近极限，为理解该模型的谱性质提供了精确结果。

Abstract: We consider the Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model of $N$ Majorana fermions with random $q$-body interactions. For $q=4$, we show that as $N\to \infty$ through even integers, the largest eigenvalue of the model satisfies
  \[
  \frac{λ_1}{\sqrt{N}}\to 4\int_0^\infty g_0(t)^4\,\mathrm{d}t \approx 0.32504 \qquad \mbox{almost surely}\,,
  \] where $g_0(t)=\frac{1}{2}\int \mathrm{e}^{-Et}ρ_0(\mathrm{d}E)$ is the unique solution of the zero-temperature quartic Schwinger--Dyson equation for which $ρ_0$ is a probability measure, $\int E^2ρ_0(\mathrm{d}E)=1/4$, and $g_0^3\in L^1(0,\infty)$. The main ingredient of the proof is the calculation of the SYK free-energy limit at every fixed positive temperature. We achieve this by introducing a new finite-bath interpolation, which reduces the quartic SYK pressure problem to a local cavity-kernel identity valid at every such temperature. We then identify the zero-temperature slope of the Schwinger--Dyson pressure and transfer it to the spectral edge.
  GPT-5.6 assisted with literature search, the development of technical arguments, and manuscript preparation; the author is responsible for the contents.

</details>


### [129] [Metric completion of the Bender--Brody--Müller Hamiltonian: dilation spectrum and missing eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19067)
*Kejun Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了BBM非厄米哈密顿量在由其候选度量η诱导的希尔伯特完备化中的性质，发现该完备化空间与L^2(R_+)酉等价，其自由自伴实现是膨胀生成子，具有纯绝对连续谱R。关键结论是：候选本征函数Δψ_z=x^{-z}在Re z=1/2时不属于完备化空间，因此原始的BBM边界条件/本征函数机制无法在此L^2基度量完备化中产生Riemann零点的点谱态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析BBM哈密顿量作为非厄米Hilbert-Pólya算子时，由其候选度量η诱导的希尔伯特完备化结构，探究该框架下能否实现Riemann零点对应的点谱。

Method: 通过分析度量η₀=Δ†Δ的正定性、非强制性及诱导范数，构造完备化空间并建立其与L^2(R_+)的酉等价性；研究自由自伴实化的谱性质；检验候选本征函数在完备化空间中的隶属关系。

Result: 1) 完备化空间与L^2(R_+)酉等价，自由自伴实化为膨胀生成子，谱为纯绝对连续R；2) 无界夹层Δ†h(D)Δ无有界逆；3) 迁移对称算子的亏指数为(∞,∞)，其伴随以每个实点为无限重特征值，而自由延拓为纯连续谱；4) 候选本征函数Δψ_z=x^{-z}在Re z=1/2时不属于完备化空间。

Conclusion: 在L^2基度量完备化框架下，原始的BBM边界条件/本征函数机制无法产生Riemann零点的点谱态。

Abstract: The Bender--Brody--Müller (BBM) Hamiltonian was proposed as a non-Hermitian Hilbert--Pólya operator. We analyze the Hilbert completion induced, on the standard half-line $L^2$ core, by BBM's candidate metric $\hatη=\sin^2(\hat p/2)=Δ^\daggerΔ/4$. The form $η_0=Δ^\daggerΔ$ is positive with trivial kernel but is not coercive. Completing $C_c^\infty(0,\infty)$ in the norm $\|ψ\|_{η_0}=\|Δψ\|$ gives a Hilbert space canonically unitarily equivalent to $L^2(\mathbb R_+)$. Its free self-adjoint realization is the dilation generator, with simple, purely absolutely continuous spectrum $\mathbb R$. The analysis yields two spectral statements of interest beyond the BBM problem. First, no bounded sandwich $Δ^\dagger h(D)Δ$ is boundedly invertible. Second, the transported symmetric operator has deficiency indices $(\infty,\infty)$ and an adjoint with every real point as an eigenvalue of infinite multiplicity, while its free extension is purely continuous. The realization-independent BBM conclusion concerns the candidate eigenfunctions: $Δψ_z=x^{-z}$, so for $\operatorname{Re}z=1/2$ they do not belong to the completed space. Thus the original BBM boundary-condition/eigenfunction mechanism cannot produce point-spectrum Riemann-zero states in this $L^2$-based metric completion.

</details>


### [130] [Lagrange points of the restricted three-body problem in spaces of constant curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19148)
*Miguel Ayala,Carlos Barrera-Anzaldo,Luis C. García-Naranjo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维常曲率空间中限制性三体问题的相对平衡点的分类与稳定性，特别聚焦于正曲率情形，通过解析方法与计算机辅助证明结合，严格确认了此前仅有数值证据的现象，并揭示了正曲率对三角平衡点L₄和L₅的稳定化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Kilin (1999)和Martínez & Simó (2017)关于常曲率空间中限制性三体问题相对平衡点的工作，旨在对正曲率情形建立严格的分类与稳定性结果，并解释正曲率、负曲率与平面情形在相对平衡点行为上的定性差异。

Method: 将问题表述为具有两个自由度的自治Lagrangian系统，参数为曲率κ和主天体质量比μ；针对κ>0情形，结合解析方法与计算机辅助证明进行分类研究。

Result: 严格确认了正曲率情形下此前仅有数值证据的现象；发现正曲率对三角平衡点L₄和L₅具有稳定化效应，而负曲率则呈现相反效果；提供了正曲率与平面及负曲率情形定性差异的拓扑解释。

Conclusion: 正曲率对三角平衡点具有稳定化作用，负曲率则去稳定化；小质量比μ regime下该效应显著；拓扑结构差异是造成不同曲率下平衡点行为定性不同的根本原因。

Abstract: We continue the study initiated by Kilin ({\em Reg. Chaot. Dyn.} 4, (1999)) and by Martínez and Simó ({\em Celest. Mech. Dynam. Astronom.} 128, (2017)) on the classification and stability of the relative equilibria of the restricted three-body problem in two-dimensional spaces of constant curvature, which generalize the classical Lagrange points of the planar problem. After formulating the problem as an autonomous Lagrangian system with two degrees of freedom, whose only parameters are the curvature $κ$ and the mass ratio $μ$ of the primaries, we establish several classification results for the case $κ>0$ by combining analytical methods with computer-assisted proofs. These results provide rigorous confirmation of phenomena for which previously only numerical evidence was available. We also provide topological explanations for the qualitative differences between the behavior of relative equilibria in positive curvature and that observed in the planar and negative-curvature cases. Our analysis focuses on the regime of small $μ$ and indicates that positive curvature has a stabilizing effect on the triangular equilibria $\Ll_4$ and $\Ll_5$, whereas negative curvature appears to have the opposite effect.

</details>


### [131] [Superintegrable systems of two interacting spin-$\frac12$ particles with first-order vector integrals of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19155)
*O. Ogulcan Tuncer,I. Yurdusen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维欧氏空间中两个自旋1/2非相对论粒子的量子超可积性，分类了所有具有非平凡一阶矢量运动积分的系统，并讨论了对称代数及标量约化导致的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前对于自旋粒子标量和赝标量一阶积分的分类工作，系统研究一阶矢量积分存在的条件。

Method: 构造最一般的厄米一阶矢量算符（由相对位置、动量、轨道角动量和两个自旋矢量构成），通过要求其与哈密顿量对易导出径向确定方程组，求解得到所有允许的势函数和对应矢量积分。

Result: 给出了所有允许的势函数和对应矢量积分的完整列表；在选定代表情形下，通过标量约化得到了精确的库仑型和谐振子型解。

Conclusion: 完成了对含自旋-轨道、自旋-自旋、张量和二次自旋-轨道相互作用的双粒子系统的一阶矢量运动积分的完整分类。

Abstract: We study quantum superintegrability for two interacting non-relativistic spin-$\frac12$ particles in three-dimensional Euclidean space. The Hamiltonian contains a central potential together with spin-orbit, spin-spin, tensor, and quadratic spin-orbit interaction terms, all depending only on the relative distance. We restrict the classification to the $V_4=0$ case, so that the spin-momentum interaction term is not included. We determine all such systems admitting non-trivial first-order vector integrals of motion. For this purpose, we construct the most general Hermitian first-order vector operator built from the relative position, momentum, orbital angular momentum, and the two spin vectors. The commutativity condition with the Hamiltonian leads to an overdetermined system of radial determining equations, whose solution gives the complete list of admissible potentials and the corresponding vector integrals within this class. The results extend the previous classifications of scalar and pseudo-scalar first-order integrals for two particles with spin. We also discuss selected symmetry algebras generated by the vector integrals and show, in one representative case, how a scalar reduction leads to exact Coulomb- and oscillator-type solutions.

</details>

### [132] [Picard groups and composition nilpotence for finite cellular isotropic spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18322)
*David Kumallagov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究模2各向同性球谱的Picard群，证明其同构于ℤ²，并建立了基于E_(**)-同调的对角权结构理论，给出了幂零理想指数上界及Fitting分解等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在flexible域（特征≠2）上，理解模2各向同性球谱的张量可逆有限胞腔谱的分类问题，以及建立相应的同调检测工具。

Method: 利用模2各向同性球谱𝕏与MBP的smash积构造谱E，通过E_(**)同调作为保守函子，结合对角权结构（diagonal weight structure）恢复精确权与最小复形项，分析End(M)到End(E∧_𝕏 M)的核的幂零性。

Result: (1) Pic(SH(k/k)^c_cell) ≅ ℤ²，即每个张量可逆有限胞腔各向同性谱都是𝕏的唯一双次平移；(2) E_(**)在有限胞腔对象上保守，对角集中迫使有限直和各向同性球谱；(3) 给出了幂零理想的指数上界d(M)(2L(M)-1)；(4) 得到了复合幂零性检测和典范Fitting分解。

Conclusion: 建立了特征≠2的flexible域上模2各向同性稳定同伦论中张量可逆对象的完全分类，并发展了基于同调检测幂零性和结构分解的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Let $k=k_0(t_1,t_2,\ldots)$ be a flexible field of characteristic different from $2$, let $\mathbb X$ be the mod-$2$ isotropic sphere, and set $E=\mathbb X \wedge MBP$. We prove
  $$Pic\bigl(SH(k/k)^c_{cell}\bigr)\cong\mathbb Z^2; $$ thus every tensor-invertible finite cellular isotropic spectrum is a unique bigraded suspension of $\mathbb X$. More generally, $E_{**}$ is conservative on finite cellular objects, and concentration on one diagonal forces a finite direct sum of suspended isotropic spheres. A bounded diagonal weight structure recovers the exact weights and minimal-complex terms from $E_{**}$. For every nonzero finite cellular $M$, the kernel of
  $$End(M)\longrightarrow End(E\wedge_{\mathbb X}M)$$ is a composition-nilpotent ideal, with exponent at most $d(M)(2L(M)-1)$, where $L(M)$ is the diagonal width and $d(M)$ the maximal number of distinct Tate degrees on one diagonal. This yields detection of composition nilpotence and canonical Fitting decompositions.

</details>


### [133] [Hyperkähler sixfolds, abelian fourfolds of Weil type and a Hodge class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18341)
*Bert van Geemen,Antonio Rapagnetta*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为具有平凡判别式的Weil型一般阿贝尔四重簇的Hodge猜想提供了另一种证明，关键是通过将其映射到K3^{[3]}型超凯勒六重簇，并利用该六重簇切丛的第二陈类的拉回作为非交积的代数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有多种方法证明了具有平凡判别式的Weil型一般阿贝尔四重簇的Hodge猜想，本文旨在提供另一种证明路径。

Method: 首先回顾Weil型阿贝尔四重簇的基本事实，然后利用超凯勒流形的双有理几何和形变理论，建立从阿贝尔四重簇到K3^{[3]}型超凯勒六重簇的映射；通过拉回该六重簇切丛的第二陈类，得到余维数为2的非交积代数类，从而证明主定理。

Result: 成功建立了阿贝尔四重簇到K3^{[3]}型超凯勒六重簇的映射，并利用拉回的切丛第二陈类得到了非交积的代数类，完成了Hodge猜想的另一种证明。

Conclusion: 本文为具有平凡判别式的Weil型一般阿贝尔四重簇的Hodge猜想提供了新的证明方法，其核心贡献在于通过超凯勒几何的构造性方法得到所需的代数类。

Abstract: There are now several proofs of the Hodge conjecture for the general abelian fourfold of Weil type with trivial discriminant. This paper provides another one. The abelian fourfolds under consideration allow a map to a hyperkähler sixfold of K3$^{[3]}$ type. The pull-back of the second Chern class of the tangent bundle of the sixfold is an algebraic class in codimension two that is not an intersection of divisor classes and the main result follows.
  After recalling the basic facts on abelian fourfolds of Weil type we establish the existence of the map using results on the birational geometry of these hyperkähler manifolds and deformation theory.

</details>


### [134] [Degenerations of the complex projective plane with only rational singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19348)
*Marcos Canedo,Giancarlo Urzúa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在假设Wahl猜想成立的前提下，对只有有理奇点的复射影平面正规退化进行了分类，除了Manetti和Hacking–Prokhorov分类的由Markov方程控制的曲面外，还发现了六个包含四个非对数典范奇点的新退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Wahl猜想（二维奇点允许有理同调盘光滑化时是加权齐次的），旨在完整分类具有仅有有理奇点的复射影平面的所有正规退化。

Method: 假设Wahl猜想成立，通过理论推导和分类方法，对复射影平面的正规退化进行系统分类，并与已知的Manetti和Hacking–Prokhorov分类结果进行对比。

Result: 分类结果包括Manetti和Hacking–Prokhorov已分类的由Markov方程控制的曲面，以及六个新的包含四个非对数典范奇点的退化。

Conclusion: 在Wahl猜想成立的条件下，完成了仅有有理奇点的复射影平面正规退化的完整分类，并发现了新的退化类型。

Abstract: Wahl's conjecture states that two-dimensional singularities admitting a rational homology disk smoothing are weighted homogeneous. Assuming the conjecture, we classify all normal degenerations of the complex projective plane with only rational singularities. They are precisely the surfaces classified by Manetti and Hacking--Prokhorov, which are controlled by the Markov equation, together with six new degenerations containing four non-log canonical singularities.

</details>


### [135] [Compactification of the fifth Painlevé foliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19285)
*Mattia Morbello*

Abstract: We extend the previous compactifications of the fifth Painlevé foliation studying the behaviour of the leaves for the time parameter $t$ being equal to $0$ or $\infty$. In particular, we find, for each boundary component, a first integral of the Hamiltonian vector field, providing an explicit description of the foliation.
