### [136] [Obstructions to embedding singular curves in toric varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18475)
*Maya Banks,Izzet Coskun,Kevin Tucker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 对于每个整数 d ≥ 2，存在一条不可约、约化的曲线，它能嵌入到 ℙ^d 中，但不能嵌入到任何维数小于 d 的射影正规环面簇中，特别是不能嵌入到任何维数小于 d 的加权射影空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线在射影空间与环面簇（如加权射影空间）中嵌入性的维数下界，探讨是否存在曲线虽能嵌入高维射影空间，却无法嵌入更低维的环面簇。

Method: 通过构造具体的不可约、约化曲线，并证明其可嵌入 ℙ^d 但无法嵌入任何维数小于 d 的射影正规环面簇（包括加权射影空间）。

Result: 对于任意 d ≥ 2，存在满足上述嵌入性条件的曲线。

Conclusion: 射影正规环面簇（包括加权射影空间）在嵌入低维曲线方面存在局限性，并非所有可嵌入 ℙ^d 的曲线都能嵌入更低维的此类簇中。

Abstract: For every integer $d \geq 2$, we show that there exists an irreducible, reduced curve which embeds in $\mathbb{P}^d$ but in no projective normal toric variety of dimension less than $d$. In particular, there exist reduced irreducible curves that embed in $\mathbb{P}^d$ but do not embed in any weighted projective space of dimension less than $d$.

</details>


### [137] [Foliations with small singular set in arbitrary characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18571)
*Thiago Fassarella,Wodson Mendson,João Pedro dos Santos,Frédéric Touzet*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意特征代数闭域上光滑代数簇叶状结构的几何性质，重点刻画了正特征情形下与零特征的异同，包括正则叶状结构的分类、Camacho-Sad指标在任意特征下的推广、以及p-闭叶状结构的Bott消失定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征代数簇上的叶状结构理论相较于复几何情形尚不完善，许多经典结果（如分类定理、消失定理）在正特征下的成立条件与局限尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过对极小有理曲面和del Pezzo曲面上正则叶状结构进行完全分类；将Camacho-Sad指标及其和公式推广到任意特征；利用不变超曲面的奇异集约束条件分析射影空间上的叶状结构；在Hodge上同调和Chow环中建立p-闭叶状结构的Bott消失定理。

Result: 正特征下极小有理曲面和del Pezzo曲面上的正则叶状结构是p-闭的，但特征2的弱del Pezzo曲面上存在非p-闭的正则叶状结构；Camacho-Sad指标和公式在任意特征下成立；射影空间上若存在奇异集足够小的不变超曲面，则叶状结构必为p-闭且法丛次数满足整除性条件；p-闭叶状结构满足Hodge上同调和Chow环中的Bott消失定理。

Conclusion: 正特征叶状结构既有与零特征相似的分类结果（如正则叶状结构的p-闭性），也有独特现象（如特征2的反例）；Camacho-Sad指标和Bott消失定理可自然推广到正特征情形，为后续研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates the geometry of foliations on smooth algebraic varieties over an algebraically closed field of arbitrary characteristic $p \ge 0$. We address several specific features of foliations in positive characteristic, aiming to highlight both similarities and differences with the characteristic zero case. First, we provide a complete classification of regular foliations on minimal rational and del Pezzo surfaces, establishing that in positive characteristic, such foliations are $p$-closed. However, there are regular foliations on weak del Pezzo surfaces, in characteristic 2, which are not $p$-closed. We extend the Camacho-Sad index and its associated sum formula to arbitrary characteristic, using it to study the behavior of foliations and distributions with small singular set. For foliations on projective spaces, we prove that the existence of an invariant hypersurface with a sufficiently small singular set forces the foliation to be $p$-closed and imposes strict divisibility conditions on the degree of its normal bundle. Finally, we establish versions of the Bott vanishing theorem in both Hodge cohomology and the Chow ring for $p$-closed foliations, providing a positive characteristic analogue to classical vanishing results in complex geometry.

</details>


### [138] [Non-liftable varieties via etale cohomology rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18588)
*Runjie Hu,Siqing Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在正特征下构造了一个光滑射影簇，其$\mathbb{Q}_{\ell}$系数的平展上同调环不是任何分次$\mathbb{Q}$-代数的标量扩张，从而展示了阻碍提升到特征零的一类新障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找并证明正特征光滑射影簇无法提升到特征零的新类型障碍，超越已知的Hodge理论或晶体上同调等障碍。

Method: 通过构造具体的正特征光滑射影簇，分析其$\mathbb{Q}_{\ell}$-平展上同调环的代数结构，证明其不能表示为某个分次$\mathbb{Q}$-代数的标量扩张（即$\mathbb{Q}_{\ell} \otimes_{\mathbb{Q}} A$形式）。

Result: 成功构造出满足上述性质的例子，表明存在一类新的、基于上同调环有理结构缺失的障碍，阻止正特征簇提升到特征零。

Conclusion: 发现了阻碍正特征簇提升到特征零的新机制，丰富了该领域障碍的分类，对理解正特征代数几何与特征零代数几何之间的差异有重要意义。

Abstract: We construct a smooth projective variety in positive characteristic whose $\mathbb{Q}_{\ell}$-coefficient etale cohomology ring is not the scalar extension of any graded $\mathbb{Q}$-algebra, providing an example of a new type of obstruction to characteristic zero liftability.

</details>


### [139] [Homological rigidity and Schur rigidity of Schubert varieties in rational homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18593)
*Cong Ding,Qifeng Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高皮卡数有理齐次空间中舒伯特变体的同调刚性（homological rigidity）和舒尔刚性（Schur rigidity）问题，证明了在长根情形（包括ADE类型）下光滑舒伯特变体具有同调刚性，给出了子图类型舒伯特变体是否具有同调刚性的完整列表，并证明了在长根情形下子图类型舒伯特变体具有舒尔刚性，除非它允许到投影空间上的纤维丛结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于有理齐次空间中舒伯特变体的同调刚性和舒尔刚性研究主要集中于皮卡数为一的情形，本文旨在将这两个刚性问题的研究推广到高皮卡数的有理齐次空间中，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 采用代数几何和表示论的方法，通过分析长根情形（包括ADE类型）以及子图类型舒伯特变体的几何结构，结合纤维丛结构等技术手段，系统研究高皮卡数情形下的同调刚性和舒尔刚性。

Result: 1. 在长根情形（包括所有G为ADE类型的情况）下，光滑舒伯特变体具有同调刚性；2. 给出了子图类型舒伯特变体是否具有同调刚性的完整列表；3. 证明了在长根情形下，子图类型舒伯特变体具有舒尔刚性，除非该变体允许到投影空间上的纤维丛结构。

Conclusion: 成功将舒伯特变体的同调刚性和舒尔刚性研究从皮卡数为一的情形推广到高皮卡数的有理齐次空间中，获得了关于长根情形和子图类型舒伯特变体的刚性表征的完整结果。

Abstract: A Schubert variety $X_0$ on a rational homogenous space $X=G/P$ is said to be homologically rigid, if any subvariety $Z$ on $X$ representing the same homology class with $X_0$ must satisfy $Z=g\cdot X_0$ for some $g\in{\rm Aut_0}(X)$. We say $X_0$ is Schur rigid, if furthermore any subvariety $Z$ on $X$ whose homology class is a multiple $r$ of that of $X_0$ must satisfy $Z=g_1\cdot X_0+\cdots+g_r\cdot X_0$ for some $g_1,\cdots ,g_r\in{\rm Aut_0}(X)$. Homological rigidity and Schur rigidity of Schubert varieties in rational homogeneous spaces of Picard number one have been well studied in extensive literature. In this paper, we study both rigidity problems of Schubert varieties in rational homogeneous spaces of higher Picard numbers. We show that in the long root cases, including all cases when $G$ is of type $ADE$, smooth Schubert varieties have homological rigidity. Besides, we give the complete list of Schubert varieties of subdiagram type with/without homological rigidity. Furthermore, for a Schubert variety $X_0$ of subdiagram type, we show that it has Schur rigidity in long root cases unless $X_0$ admits a fiber bundle structure over the projective space.

</details>


### [140] [On the Toda hierarchy for the stationary theory of the tube with negative contact orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18656)
*Hsian-Hua Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构建了带负接触的$( \PP^1,0,\infty)$平稳相对理论的master $2$-Toda tau序列，负接触块由无穷楔李代数元素的有限乘积编码，在标准$Q,u$特化下，其正符号度系数恢复平稳相对Gromov-Witten不变量，并受Hirota方程约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为带负接触的平稳相对Gromov-Witten理论建立完整的可积系统描述，特别是通过$2$-Toda层次结构统一处理正负接触信息。

Method: 利用负接触的大根定义、Johnson-Kumaran-Wu算子公式和京都/Plücker构造，将负接触块编码为无穷楔李代数元素的有限乘积的指数，在$\infty$处的块取为左块的伴随；通过邻接独立的正profile Toda时间，使带电真空矩阵元构成$2$-Toda tau序列。

Result: 构造的master tau序列大于枚举扇区，包含非零非真空的零符号度系数和辅助非零晶格分量；正符号度系数在标准$Q,u$特化下恢复平稳相对Gromov-Witten不变量；Hirota方程构成耦合master系统，不额外输入则无法仅对正度不变量封闭。

Conclusion: 该master $2$-Toda tau序列为带负接触的平稳相对理论提供了完整的可积结构框架，但枚举不变量仅为其正度部分，全序列包含更丰富的非枚举信息。

Abstract: We construct a master $2$-Toda tau sequence for the stationary relative theory of $(\PP^1,0,\infty)$ with any fixed finite lists of negative contacts. The negative-contact blocks are encoded by finite products of exponentials of infinite-wedge Lie algebra elements, with the block at $\infty$ taken as the adjoint of the entire corresponding left block. After adjoining independent positive-profile Toda times, the resulting charged-vacuum matrix elements form a genuine $2$-Toda tau sequence. Following the standard $Q,u$ specialization, its positive signed-degree coefficients recover the stationary relative Gromov--Witten invariants with the prescribed positive and negative contacts, which occur in and are constrained by the induced coefficientwise Hirota equations.
  The master tau sequence is larger than the enumerative sector: it generally contains nonzero, non-vacuum signed-degree-zero coefficients, as well as auxiliary nonzero lattice components, for which no enumerative interpretation is asserted. Consequently the Hirota equations form a coupled master system and do not, without additional input, close on the positive-degree invariants alone. The construction uses the large-root definition of negative contact, the Johnson--Kumaran--Wu operator formula for the tube, and the Kyoto/Plücker construction of the $2$-Toda hierarchy.

</details>


### [141] [Semipositivity of the orbifold second Chern class in Fujiki's class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18699)
*Masataka Iwai,Satoshi Jinnouchi,Shiyu Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fujiki类中紧致正规解析簇的orbifold第二陈类不等式，证明了具有nef典范除子的奇异簇的Miyaoka不等式，以及具有nef反典范除子的簇的orbifold第二陈类半正定性，并建立了切丛和余切丛的generic nefness定理及混合极化的orbifold Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典代数几何中关于光滑簇的陈类不等式推广到Fujiki类中的奇异解析簇上，特别是Miyaoka不等式和Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式在orbifold（轨形）情形下的推广。

Method: 通过建立切丛和余切丛的generic nefness定理，以及发展混合极化下的orbifold Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式，作为核心技术工具来证明主要结果。

Result: 成功证明了Fujiki类中具有nef典范除子的奇异簇满足Miyaoka不等式；对于具有nef反典范除子的簇，orbifold第二陈类是半正定的。

Conclusion: 将Miyaoka不等式和Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式等经典结果从光滑情形推广到了Fujiki类中的奇异解析簇，丰富了高维复几何与奇点理论中的不等式理论。

Abstract: We study inequalities for orbifold second Chern classes of compact normal analytic varieties in Fujiki's class. We prove Miyaoka's inequality for singular varieties in Fujiki's class with nef canonical divisor, as well as the semipositivity of the orbifold second Chern class for varieties with nef anti-canonical divisor. To prove these results, we establish generic nefness theorems for tangent and cotangent sheaves and an orbifold Bogomolov--Gieseker inequality for mixed polarizations.

</details>


### [142] [The modulo eight behavior of common factors of two hypergeometric polynomials arising from a genus three heperelliptic family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18717)
*Youhei Morita*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究了一类特征为奇数的代数闭域上的单参数超椭圆曲线族，通过Cartier算子将超特殊性转化为两个截断超几何多项式的同时消失，证明了其首一最大公因式是无平方的且每个不可约因子次数至多为二，并根据特征模8的剩余类确定了其行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超椭圆曲线的超特殊性在特征p域上的代数结构，特别是通过截断超几何多项式建立与Cartier算子的联系。

Method: 将超特殊性条件转化为两个截断超几何多项式的联立零点问题，分析它们的首一最大公因式的代数性质（无平方性、因子次数界限），并依据特征模8的剩余类进行分类讨论。

Result: 首一最大公因式无平方；每个不可约因子次数≤2；特征模8的一个剩余类中所有非常数不可约因子都是二次的；两个剩余类中公因式平凡；一个剩余类中总出现一个一次因子。

Conclusion: 超椭圆曲线的超特殊性可通过截断超几何多项式的消失性刻画，且其代数结构完全由特征模8的剩余类决定。

Abstract: We study a one-parameter family of smooth projective genus-three hyperelliptic curves over algebraically closed fields of odd characteristic, with the parameter different from zero and one. We translate superspeciality into the simultaneous vanishing of two truncated hypergeometric polynomials arising from the Cartier operator. For their monic greatest common divisor, we prove squarefreeness and show that every irreducible factor has degree at most two. We then determine its behavior according to the characteristic modulo eight: in one residue class every nonconstant irreducible factor is quadratic; in two residue classes the greatest common divisor is trivial; and in the remaining residue class a distinguished linear factor always occurs.

</details>


### [143] [On degenerate geometric rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18898)
*Matěj Doležálek,Paweł Pielasa,Derek Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了退化几何秩张量的提升问题，给出了不可提升的充分判据，刻画了由秩1退化引起的张量特征，并提供了源于非线性轨迹的新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解退化几何秩张量的结构性质，特别是何时不能提升到更高维张量，以及退化根源在于秩1轨迹还是非线性轨迹。

Method: 理论分析与判据证明，结合具体张量实例构造与分类。

Result: 给出了张量不可提升的充分判据；刻画了由秩1轨迹导致退化的张量类型；发现了源于非线性轨迹的新退化张量实例。

Conclusion: 退化几何秩张量的提升障碍与退化来源有明确的理论刻画，且存在非线性轨迹引发的退化现象。

Abstract: A tensor of degenerate geometric rank corresponds to a linear space of matrices in which some locus of matrices of rank at most $r$ has unexpectedly large dimension. We prove a sufficient criterion for when such a tensor cannot be lifted to a larger tensor and characterize those tensors for which the degeneracy is caused by the rank $r=1$ locus. We also provide new examples of tensors with degenerate geometric rank stemming from nonlinear loci.

</details>


### [144] [Zero-cycles on surfaces dominated by products of hyperelliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18906)
*Jean-Louis Colliot-Thélène,Federico Scavia,Alexei Skorobogatov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在相关Tate-Shafarevich群有限的条件下，证明了由y² = f₁(x₁)f₂(x₂)给出的曲面上1次零循环的局部-整体结果，其中f₁和f₂是代数一般的多项式。证明结合了纤维化方法、二次扭转族中2-Selmer群的奇偶性结果，以及Morgan定理关于Cassels-Tate配对变化的一个变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算术几何中，局部-整体原理（Hasse原理）对于代数簇上的零循环是否成立是一个基本问题。本文致力于研究一类特定曲面上的1次零循环的局部-整体性质。

Method: 证明结合了三种技术：纤维化方法、二次扭转族中2-Selmer群的奇偶性结果，以及Morgan定理关于Cassels-Tate配对变化的一个变体。

Result: 在相关Tate-Shafarevich群有限的条件下，证明了由y² = f₁(x₁)f₂(x₂)给出的曲面上1次零循环的局部-整体结果。

Conclusion: 在适当的有限性假设下，这类曲面上的1次零循环满足局部-整体原理，且证明方法展示了纤维化方法与Selmer群奇偶性分析的结合有效性。

Abstract: Conditionally on the finiteness of the relevant Tate-Shafarevich groups, we prove a local-to-global result for zero-cycles of degree $1$ on the surfaces given by $y^2 = f_1(x_1)f_2(x_2)$, where the polynomials $f_1$ and $f_2$ are algebraically general. The proof combines the fibration method, parity results for $2$-Selmer groups in quadratic twist families, and a variant of a theorem of Morgan on the variation of the Cassels-Tate pairing.

</details>


### [145] [Topology and geometry of moduli spaces of semistable sheaves on bielliptic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18918)
*Aleksei Piskunov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双椭圆曲面上半稳定层的模空间的拓扑与低度Hodge理论。对于原始秩零的Mukai向量，其固定行列式分量有一个到|L|的支撑态射，可解释为相对紧化Jacobian。利用该纤维化、正线性系统的Lefschetz性质、单值化与混合Hodge结构，构造了从π₁(S)到π₁(M)的满同态，计算了Albanese纤维F、M及M°的前两个Betti数。通过Fourier-Mukai变换和Bridgeland壁穿越将计算推广到正秩原始可容许Mukai向量。进一步证明了F的第二上同调为纯(1,1)型，并在特定条件下F是严格不可约Calabi-Yau簇，M°非典范地双有理于Pic⁰(S)×Hilb^{v²/2}(S)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统理解双椭圆曲面上半稳定层模空间的拓扑不变量（基本群、Betti数）与Hodge结构，揭示其与基曲面S的深层联系，并推广到正秩情形。

Method: 利用固定行列式分量到完备线性系统|L|的支撑态射作为相对紧化Jacobian的纤维化；运用Lefschetz型性质、单值化作用和混合Hodge结构分析；结合Fourier-Mukai变换与Bridgeland壁穿越技术从秩零情形推广到正秩情形。

Result: 构造了从S的基本群到模空间基本群的满同态；计算了Albanese纤维F、模空间M及其特殊分量M°的前两个Betti数；证明了F的H²为纯(1,1)型；在特定条件下F是严格不可约Calabi-Yau簇；M°非典范地双有理于Pic⁰(S)×Hilb^{v²/2}(S)。

Conclusion: 双椭圆曲面上的半稳定层模空间具有丰富的拓扑结构，其基本群继承自基曲面S，Betti数可精确计算，且与Hilbert概形和Picard簇存在双有理等价关系，深化了对这类模空间几何的理解。

Abstract: We study the topology and low-degree Hodge theory of moduli spaces of semistable sheaves on bielliptic surfaces. For primitive rank-zero Mukai vectors $\mathbf{v}=(0,c_1(L),χ)$ satisfying the positivity condition $\operatorname{nt}(L)\geq3$, the distinguished fixed-determinant component $M_{H,S}(\mathbf{v},L)$ admits a support morphism to $|L|$ and is interpreted as a relative compactified Jacobian. Using this fibration, Lefschetz-type properties of positive linear systems, monodromy, and mixed Hodge structures, we construct a surjective homomorphism $ π_1(S)\twoheadrightarrowπ_1\bigl(M_{H,S}(\mathbf{v},L)\bigr) $ and compute the first two Betti numbers of an Albanese fiber $F$, $M_{H,S}(\mathbf{v},L)$, and the distinguished component $M^\circ_{H,S}(\mathbf{v})$. Fourier-Mukai transforms and Bridgeland wall crossing extend these computations to primitive admissible Mukai vectors of positive rank. We further prove that $H^2(F,\mathbb{C})$ is of pure Hodge type $(1,1)$. If $λ_S=\ell(\mathbf{v})=1,$ then $F$ is a strict irreducible Calabi-Yau variety up to a finite quasi-étale cover. Under the additional genericity assumption on the pullback polarization, $M^\circ_{H,S}(\mathbf{v})$ is smooth and is noncanonically birational to $\operatorname{Pic}^0(S)\times\operatorname{Hilb}^{\mathbf{v}^2/2}(S)$.

</details>


### [146] [On the Fields of Moduli of Curves of Genus Six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18992)
*Tianzhi Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征闭域上亏格6曲线的模域问题，通过分析模空间M_6的分层结构，探讨曲线何时能在其模域上定义模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本问题是确定代数闭域上的簇何时能定义在其模域上，本文专门针对亏格6曲线这一具体情形进行研究。

Method: 通过考虑模空间M_6的分层结构来分析问题。

Result: 给出了亏格6曲线能否定义在模域上的条件刻画。

Conclusion: 对亏格6曲线的模域问题给出了系统的分层分析结果。

Abstract: The field of moduli of a variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field $K$ is defined as the fixed field of those automorphisms $σ$ of $K$ for which $X\cong X^σ$. A fundamental question is under what conditions a variety admits a model over its field of moduli. We investigate this problem for curves of genus $6$, by considering the stratification of the moduli space $M_6$.

</details>


### [147] [Topology of higher Albanese maps and aspherical varieties with nilpotent fundamental group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19013)
*Vasily Rogov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对正规复代数簇X，从其次s阶中心商群的整数同调到X的整数同调的映射在超过某个次数后消失，并推论出如果基本群是虚拟幂零的，则实际上是虚拟二步幂零的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Aguilar-Campana关于aspherical簇的一个问题：当基本群是虚拟幂零时，它是否一定是虚拟二步幂零的。

Method: 利用Morgan-Hain Hodge滤过、higher Albanese流形的q-凸性以及higher Albanese映射的可定义性。

Result: 自然映射H^k(𝒢^s_ℤ(X), ℤ)→H^k(X, ℤ)在k>dim F^1𝔤^s时消失；若基本群虚拟幂零则虚拟二步幂零。

Conclusion: 对aspherical正规复代数簇，给出了Aguilar-Campana问题在基本群虚拟幂零情形下的肯定回答。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a normal complex algebraic variety. Let $\mathcal{G}^s_{\mathbb{Z}}(X)$ be the maximal torsion free nilpotent quotient of $π_1(X)$ of nilpotency class at most $s$. Let $F^{\bullet}\mathfrak{g}^s$ be the Morgan--Hain Hodge filtration on the Lie algebra of the $s$-th lower central quotient of the complex Malcev completion of $π_1(X)$. We show that the natural map $H^k(\mathcal{G}^s_{\mathbb{Z}}(X), \mathbb{Z}) \to H^k(X, \mathbb{Z})$ vanishes for $k > \dim F^1\mathfrak{g}^s$. If $\mathcal{G}^s_{\mathbb{Z}}(X)$ is of nilpotency class greater than two, this includes the top nonvanishing degree of $H^{\bullet}(\mathcal{G}^s_{\mathbb{Z}}(X), \mathbb{Z})$. We deduce that if the fundamental group of an aspherical normal variety is virtually nilpotent, it is virtually two-step nilpotent. This gives a positive answer to a question of Aguilar and Campana in this case of aspherical varieties. The ingredients of the proof are the $q$-convexity of higher Albanese manifolds and the definability of higher Albanese maps.

</details>


### [148] [A counter-example to Batyrev's conjecture on the non-negativity of stringy Hodge numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19184)
*Matthew Satriano,Jeremy Usatine*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过AI辅助发现了一个Batyrev猜想的反例，该猜想认为弦霍奇数非负，是 motivic 积分、镜像对称和McKay对应等领域的重要未解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Batyrev关于弦霍奇数非负性的猜想是数学领域一个长期悬而未决的基本问题，对motivic积分、镜像对称和McKay对应等方向具有重要指导意义。作者试图验证或反驳这一猜想。

Method: 借助OpenAI的ChatGPT进行辅助计算和探索，通过AI发现潜在反例，再经过严格的数学验证确认。

Result: 成功构造了一个Batyrev猜想的反例，即存在某些代数簇使得其弦霍奇数出现负值，从而否定了该猜想的普遍正确性。

Conclusion: Batyrev关于弦霍奇数非负性的猜想不成立，本文给出的反例终结了这一长期悬而未决的问题，同时展示了AI辅助数学研究的潜力。

Abstract: Batyrev's conjecture on the non-negativity of stringy Hodge numbers has been a fundamental open problem, guiding and motivating many beautiful mathematical results in motivic integration, mirror symmetry, and the McKay correspondence. We give a counter-example to Batyrev's conjecture which was discovered with the assistance of OpenAI's ChatGPT.

</details>


### [149] [Basic properties of kappa classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19251)
*Valery Alexeev*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在KSBA模空间上定义了kappa类（推广了曲线模空间上的Miller-Morita-Mumford类），证明了其底变换相容性、乘积与规范化公式、互反函子性、变分以上的消失性，并揭示了kappa类与变分、Chern类及operational墙穿越之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将曲线模空间上经典的Miller-Morita-Mumford (MMM) kappa类推广到更高维的KSBA模空间（即广义稳定对/奇异稳定变体的模空间）上，建立相应的操作型Chow上同调理论。

Method: 在KSBA模堆叠的操作型Chow上同调中定义kappa类；通过底变换相容性、乘积与规范化公式、互反函子性等公理化性质构建理论框架；利用变分(normalized variation)分析kappa类的非负性、消失性和数值非平凡性；研究边界系数变化下的operational墙穿越行为；证明每个正次数kappa类是某个自然虚拟向量丛的单一Chern类的有理倍数。

Result: 建立了kappa类的底变换相容性、乘积与规范化公式、互反函子性；证明了所有高于变分的kappa多项式均消失；高次kappa类在有效环上非负；数值非平凡的kappa类最大指标等于规范化变分；第一kappa类检测总变分；边界系数变化时kappa类呈分片多项式且满足operational墙穿越；每个正次数kappa类都是单一Chern类的有理倍数。

Conclusion: 本文成功将MMM kappa类推广至KSBA模空间，并揭示了kappa类与变分、虚拟向量丛Chern类之间的深刻联系，为高维模空间的操作型Chow理论提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We define kappa classes on KSBA moduli stacks as classes in operational Chow cohomology. They generalize the Miller-Morita-Mumford classes on the moduli spaces of curves. We prove base-change compatibility, product and normalization formulas, and crepant functoriality, together with the vanishing of all kappa polynomials above the variation. The higher kappa classes are nonnegative on effective cycles, and the largest index of a numerically nontrivial kappa class equals the normalized variation, while the first kappa class detects the total variation. As the boundary coefficients vary, the classes are chamberwise polynomial and compatible under operational wall crossing. Finally, every positive-degree kappa class is a rational multiple of a single Chern class of a natural virtual vector bundle.

</details>


### [150] [Finite Group Reduction of the DR/DZ Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16607)
*Si-Qi Liu,Youjin Zhang,Tianwen Zhong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了半简单CohFT的Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构的有限群约化保持双哈密顿结构和tau结构。将该结果应用于以$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$为靶的orbifold Gromov-Witten理论，发现与Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形结构相关联的两个不同的有趣可积层次，其中一个等价于亏格$1$的拓扑递归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半简单CohFT的Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构的有限群约化是否保持其双哈密顿结构和tau结构，并探索与Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上Frobenius流形结构相关的可积层次。

Method: 通过有限群约化方法分析Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构的结构保持性质，并将理论结果应用于orbifold Gromov-Witten理论的具体实例（靶空间$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$）。

Result: 有限群约化保持了双哈密顿结构和tau结构；在$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$的orbifold Gromov-Witten理论中，发现了两个不同的可积层次与$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形结构相关联，其中一个等价于亏格$1$的拓扑递归。

Conclusion: 证明了半简单CohFT的Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构在有限群约化下保持关键结构，并揭示了Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形与可积层次及拓扑递归之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We show that the finite group reduction of the Dubrovin-Zhang/Double Ramification hierarchy associated to a semisimple CohFT preserves its bihamiltonian structure and its tau structure. By applying this result to the orbifold Gromov-Witten theory with the target $\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$, we see that there are two different and interesting integrable hierarchies that can be associated to the Frobenius manifold structure on the Hurwitz space $M_{1;1}$. One of them is equivalent to the genus $1$ topological recursion.

</details>


### [151] [Isotrivial smooth curves on surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19256)
*Xi Chen,Frank Gounelas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在Picard秩为1、由无基点的丰富线束生成的平滑射影非单有理曲面上，不存在原始极化的等平凡光滑曲线族覆盖该曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲面是否可以被等平凡曲线族覆盖的问题，特别是在Picard秩为1的非单有理曲面上，探索原始极化下的几何约束。

Method: 通过代数几何方法，利用曲面的Picard秩为1、非单有理性质以及线束的丰富性与无基点性，结合等平凡族的结构特点进行证明。

Result: 证明了上述曲面不能被原始极化的等平凡光滑曲线族覆盖。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了非单有理曲面在原始极化条件下关于等平凡曲线族覆盖的一个几何限制，深化了对曲面几何结构的理解。

Abstract: We prove that a smooth projective non-uniruled surface of Picard rank one, generated by an ample and base point free line bundle, cannot be covered by an isotrivial family of smooth curves in the primitive polarisation.

</details>


### [152] [Stacky Framework for the Classification of Singular Fibers in Fibered Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19347)
*Javier Reyes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种利用根栈恰当性准则来分类光滑纤维化曲面中奇异纤维的策略，为Dokchitser的约化类型分类提供了独立于Winter抽象结果的另一种验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为光滑纤维化曲面中的奇异纤维分类提供新的理论框架，并独立验证Dokchitser的约化类型分类。

Method: 利用驯顺情况下，具有光滑一般纤维（属g>1）的纤维化对应于从栈底到\overline{\mathcal{M}}_g的可表示态射这一事实，结合根栈的恰当性准则。

Result: 建立了新的分类框架，独立验证了Dokchitser的约化类型分类。

Conclusion: 该栈论方法为奇异纤维分类提供了另一种理论支撑，不依赖于Winter的抽象结果。

Abstract: We develop a strategy for the classification of singular fibers in smooth fibered surfaces by exploiting the fact that, in the tame case, a fibration with smooth general fiber of genus $g > 1$ corresponds to a representable morphism from a stacky base to $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_g$ thanks to the root stack valuative criterion for properness of tame stacks. This framework serves as another justification of Dokchitser's classification of reduction types independently of Winters' abstract result.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Heaps of modules: homological aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18328)
*Yongduo Wang,Chenyu Wang,Jian He,Dejun Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在truss的T-模堆范畴中定义了投射对象和Gorenstein投射对象，证明了堆的投射性等价于其关联R(T)-模的投射性，以及BP Gorenstein投射性的等价刻画，并给出了BP Gorenstein投射维数的函子描述，最后证明了单值truss T是Gorenstein truss当且仅当R(T)是Iwanaga-Gorenstein环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典模论中的投射对象和Gorenstein投射对象概念推广到堆（heap）范畴中，具体是在truss的T-模堆结构中建立相应的同调理论。

Method: 通过构造从T-模堆到其关联R(T)-模的函子G_e，利用该函子将堆的（Gorenstein）投射性转化为经典模范畴中模的相应性质，并借助同调代数工具刻画投射维数。

Result: (1) T-模堆P是投射的当且仅当对所有e_p∈P，G_{e_p}(P)是投射R(T)-模；(2) T-模堆M是BP Gorenstein投射的当且仅当对所有e_m∈M，G_{e_m}(M)是Gorenstein投射R(T)-模；(3) 给出了BP Gorenstein投射维数的函子描述；(4) 单值truss T是Gorenstein truss当且仅当R(T)是Iwanaga-Gorenstein环。

Conclusion: 成功将投射与Gorenstein投射概念从模范畴推广到堆范畴，建立了堆的同调理论与经典模范畴同调理论之间的桥梁，并给出了truss的Gorenstein性与其关联环的Gorenstein性之间的等价刻画。

Abstract: The definitions of projective objects and Gorenstein projective objects in the category of heaps of $T$-modules are posed, where $T$ is a truss. It is shown that a heap of $T$-modules $P$ is projective if and only if $\mathcal{G}_{e_p}(P)$ is a projective $R(T)$-module for all $e_p\in P$ and a heap of $T$-modules $M$ is BP Gorenstein projective if and only if $\mathcal{G}_{e_m}(M)$ is a Gorenstein projective $R(T)$-module for all $e_m\in M$. Moreover, we give a functorial description of the BP Gorenstein projective dimension. Finally, it is also proven that a unital truss $T$ is a Gorenstein truss if and only if $R(T)$ is an Iwanaga-Gorenstein ring.

</details>


### [154] [Cyclic covers and non-orbit 3-representation-finite symmetric algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18497)
*Tor Kringeland*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在特征零的代数闭域上，构造了两个3-表示有限但不是轨道代数的对称代数，其中一个36维（Q(3A)^2_2的升维）和一个84维的3-球面带权曲面代数，通过幂等导子和箭头特征群排除了所有可能的二重覆盖，否定了Darpö和Iyama的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找不是轨道代数的3-表示有限对称代数，回答Darpö和Iyama关于此类代数是否必然为轨道代数的开放问题。

Method: 1) 构造Q(3A)^2_2在特征零的36维升维A，显式构造3-丛-倾斜模；2) 证明连通对称代数的轨道表示迫使代数或其连通循环覆盖具有半维平方零分次，该分次由幂等导子检测，有限群箭头特征约束可能覆盖；3) 对A仅剩三个双覆盖候选，用导子障碍排除所有候选；4) 类似方法分析84维3-球面带权曲面代数。

Result: 构造了两个3-表示有限对称代数（36维和84维），证明它们都不是任何有限维代数的轨道代数（无论整体维数如何），从而否定Darpö和Iyama问题中轨道代数刻画的可能性。

Conclusion: 存在非轨道代数的3-表示有限对称代数，轨道表示的条件（半维平方零分次、幂等导子、箭头特征群）过于严格，所构造的反例表明3-表示有限对称代数的分类需超出轨道代数范畴。

Abstract: Over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero, we exhibit two symmetric algebras that are 3-representation-finite but are not orbit algebras of repetitive categories. The first is a 36-dimensional characteristic-zero lift $A$ of $Q(3A)^2_2$, the quaternion-type algebra that Böhmler and Marczinzik proved 3-representation-finite in characteristic 2; we construct an explicit 3-cluster-tilting module. We show that any orbit presentation of a connected symmetric algebra forces the algebra, or a connected cyclic cover of it, to admit a half-dimensional square-zero grading. Such gradings are detected by idempotent derivations, and a finite group of arrow characters constrains the possible covers. For $A$, only three double-cover candidates remain and the derivation obstruction excludes all of them. Thus $A$ is not an orbit algebra of any finite-dimensional algebra, regardless of global dimension, answering a question of Darpö and Iyama. The 84-dimensional 3-spherical weighted surface algebra is likewise 3-representation-finite and not an orbit algebra.

</details>


### [155] [A Counterexample to Wehlau's Conjecture on Noether Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18585)
*Muhammad Fazeel Anwar*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在特征2的域上构造了反例，否证了Wehlau关于Noether数在子模包含下单调性的猜想。具体地，对二面体群D_8，找到了一个5维子模U包含于6维模V，使得U的不变量环的Noether数（6）大于V的（5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wehlau猜想：对于有限群G的G-模包含U⊆V，Noether数满足β(k[U]^G) ≤ β(k[V]^G)。本文旨在检验该猜想的正确性。

Method: 在特征2的域上，以二面体群D_8为研究对象，构造一个具体的6维G-模V及其5维子模U，通过计算两者不变量环的Noether数，比较大小关系。

Result: 构造的U⊆V满足β(k[U]^{D_8})=6 > 5=β(k[V]^{D_8})，直接否证了Wehlau猜想。该反例在F_2上定义，且在特征2的任意域上有效。通过提升(inflation)，任何以D_8为商群的有限群均可获得反例。

Conclusion: Wehlau关于Noether数在子模包含下单调性的猜想在特征2下不成立。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group, let $V$ be a finite-dimensional $G$-module over a field $k$, and let $U$ be a $G$-submodule of $V$. Wehlau conjectured that the corresponding Noether numbers satisfy $β\bigl(k[U]^G\bigr)\leq β\bigl(k[V]^G\bigr)$. We disprove this conjecture in characteristic $2$. For the dihedral group $D_8$, we construct an inclusion $U\subseteq V$, with $\dim_kU=5$ and $\dim_kV=6$, such that $β\bigl(k[U]^{D_8}\bigr)=6>5=β\bigl(k[V]^{D_8}\bigr)$. The construction is defined over $\mathbb F_2$ and remains valid over every field of characteristic $2$. By inflation, it yields counterexamples for every finite group admitting $D_8$ as a quotient.

</details>


### [156] [Hopf $2$-cocycles for certain affine algebraic reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18599)
*Shlomo Gelaki*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文分类了仿射代数约化群G（其单位连通分支为环面T）上的（极小）Hopf 2-上循环。通过利用ENO的命题5.4，分类了Rep(T)^K ≃ Rep(G)上的有限不可分解半单模范畴，进而分类Rep(G)上的秩1模范畴，证明对应的纤维函子是经典的（保持维数），从而与G的Hopf 2-上循环一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受仿射代数约化群G上Hopf 2-上循环分类这一开放问题的驱动，特别是文献EG1中关于极小Hopf 2-上循环的问题7.2的启发。

Method: 利用ENO的命题5.4，将问题转化为分类Rep(T)^K ≃ Rep(G)上的有限不可分解半单模范畴，然后分类Rep(G)上秩1的模范畴，并证明其对应的纤维函子是经典的（保持维数）。

Result: 成功分类了G（其单位连通分支为环面T）上的（极小）Hopf 2-上循环，并证明这些纤维函子与G的Hopf 2-上循环一一对应。

Conclusion: 对于单位连通分支为环面的仿射代数约化群，其上的Hopf 2-上循环可以被完全分类，且对应的纤维函子保持维数（经典的），从而建立了与Hopf 2-上循环的双射关系。

Abstract: Motivated by the open problem of classifying Hopf $2$-cocycles for affine algebraic reductive groups $G$ over $\mathbb{C}$, in particular by \cite[Question 7.2]{EG1} which concerns minimal Hopf $2$-cocycles, we classify (minimal) Hopf $2$-cocycles for affine algebraic reductive groups $G$ whose connected component of the identity is a torus $T$. To achieve this we utilize \cite[Proposition 5.4]{ENO} in our situation to classify finite indecomposable semisimple module categories over the infinite semisimple equivariantization tensor category $\Rep(T)^K\simeq \Rep(G)$, where $K:=G/T$. We then use it to classify the module categories over $\Rep(G)$ of rank $1$, and show that the corresponding fiber functors on $\Rep(G)$ are classical (that is, preserve dimensions), which implies that they are in bijection with Hopf $2$-cocycles for $G$.

</details>


### [157] [On the absolute convergence of the spectral side of the twisted trace formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18870)
*Werner Müller,Satoshi Wakatsuki*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了约化代数群扭曲迹公式谱侧的绝对收敛性，将Finis-Lapid-Müller的经典结果推广到扭曲情形，为自守表示理论提供了严格的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该定理的证明在专家圈内已知但未在文献中详细发表，而这一结果对极限重数问题和GL_n自对偶自守表示的Weyl律等应用至关重要。

Method: 本文通过详细推导，将Finis-Lapid-Müller关于非扭曲迹公式谱侧绝对收敛的证明技术系统性地推广到扭曲设置中，给出了完整的证明细节。

Result: 成功建立了扭曲迹公式谱侧的绝对收敛性，填补了文献中的证明空白。

Conclusion: 该结果为极限重数问题和自对偶自守表示的Weyl律等应用奠定了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We establish the absolute convergence of the spectral side of the twisted trace formula for reductive algebraic groups. More precisely, we extend the absolute convergence theorem of the spectral side due to Finis--Lapid--Müller to the twisted setting. This paper provides a detailed proof of the absolute convergence theorem, whose proof is known to experts but does not seem to have appeared in the literature. The resulting formulas are motivated by applications to limit multiplicity problems and to Weyl laws for self-dual automorphic representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n$.

</details>


### [158] [Generic polar divisors and flag residues for root-system zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18945)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可约晶体学根系Φ的Komori-Matsumoto-Tsumurazeta函数Z_Φ(s)的极点和留数结构。证明了每个适当支撑超平面H_{S,ℓ}在一般点处是真正的极除子，推导了递归旗留数、分量质量伽马因子以及横向切片上Laurent系数的完备公式，并恢复和推广了多种已知的经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和推广多重zeta函数的极点与留数结构，建立系统性的代数框架以统一处理不同根系下的奇异数据。

Method: 利用极大支撑奇妙模型上的旗状分解，结合递推关系和代数组合方法，分析超平面除子的极点和留数，推导横向切片上的Laurent系数公式。

Result: 证明了每个适当支撑超平面H_{S,ℓ}是真正极除子；推导了递归旗留数和分量质量伽马因子；给出了Laurent系数的完备公式，其中极点阶数由非零聚合系数决定而非单个旗的最大阶数；恢复并推广了A_2、A_3、C_2、G_2等经典结果，以及B_3和C_3的正对角留数和载流子几何结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为多重zeta函数的奇异分析提供了统一的代数框架，揭示了极点结构与根系组合几何之间的深层联系，并验证了负半整数是唯一可能的二重极点位置。

Abstract: Let $Φ$ be an irreducible crystallographic root system and let $Z_Φ(\mathbf{s})$ be the untwisted Komori-Matsumoto-Tsumura zeta function of $Φ$, with one complex exponent for each positive coroot; its diagonal specialization is the single-variable Witten zeta function. For a nonempty set $S$ of simple nodes and an integer $\ell\ge 0$, let $H_{S,\ell}$ be the hyperplane on which the exponents of the roots meeting $S$ sum to $|S|-\ell$. We prove that every proper-support hyperplane $H_{S,\ell}$ is a genuine polar divisor at a generic point, while exact homogeneity leaves only the unshifted full-support divisor; each generic residue is an explicit finite Taylor-jet sum of projective periods and polynomially weighted zeta functions of complementary subsystems. On the maximal support wonderful model, boundary contributions are indexed by strict decorated flags. We derive recursive flag residues, component-mass gamma factors, and an incidence-complete formula for the Laurent coefficients on any transverse slice: the pole order equals the largest order with nonzero aggregate coefficient, not the largest order of an individual flag. The general formulas recover the classical $A_2$ and $A_3$ singular data, Zhao's Euler-Zagier residue formulas and the Akiyama-Egami-Tanigawa lists, and the $C_2$ and $G_2$ residue functions. For $B_3$ and $C_3$ we derive the carrier geometry and lower-rank factorizations of the positive diagonal residues, identify the three-term cancellation at $s=1/8$, and show that negative half-integers are the only possible double-pole locations, recovering the known $B_3$ double coefficient at $s=-1/2$.

</details>


### [159] [Archimedean Modular Symbols for Rankin-Selberg Convolutions of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ and $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19133)
*Yubo Jin,Zeyu Tian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 和 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$ 的 Rankin-Selberg 卷积的阿基米德模符号，适用于所有一般上同调表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿基米德模符号是理解 L-函数特殊值和 p-adic 性质的关键工具，但目前对于高秩群（如 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$）的显式计算公式尚不充分。本文旨在填补这一空白，为 Rankin-Selberg 卷积提供完整的阿基米德模符号显式表达式。

Method: 利用一般上同调表示的理论，通过调和分析和表示论方法，对 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 和 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$ 的 Rankin-Selberg 卷积进行精确的模符号计算。

Result: 获得了上述两种 Rankin-Selberg 卷积情况下阿基米德模符号的完整显式公式，覆盖了所有一般上同调表示的情形。

Conclusion: 本文给出的显式公式为后续研究高秩群 L-函数的算术性质提供了基础工具，特别是在构造 p-adic L-函数和证明 Bloch-Kato 猜想等方向具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We explicit calculate the archimedean modular symbol for Rankin-Selberg convolutions of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ and $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$, for all generic cohomological representations.

</details>

### [160] [Hawking emission of massive vector fields by Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18395)
*Marco Calzà,Miguel Faria,Yuber F. Perez-Gonzalez,João G. Rosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了旋转Kerr黑洞发射有质量矢量（Proca）场的霍金辐射谱，确定了灰体因子、质量和自旋损失函数。研究发现：标量（纵向）极化模式在零质量极限下趋近于自由标量场的谱，但有限质量时差异显著；两个矢量（横向）极化模式在零质量极限下与Maxwell场结果一致。黑洞蒸发速率在慢旋时由标量模主导，接近极端时由矢量模主导。霍金温度满足$T_H\lesssim |μ-Ω_H|$时发射受到玻尔兹曼抑制，高自旋黑洞能以低于场质量的温度高效发射有质量矢量场。超辐射放大因子对有质量矢量场可达约7%（质量为零的光子约为4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前旋转黑洞的有质量矢量场霍金辐射谱尚未被计算，已有研究仅覆盖标量场、中微子场、光子场和引力子场。由于有质量矢量场在粒子物理标准模型扩展和暗物质候选者中具有重要地位，计算其霍金辐射谱对于理解黑洞蒸发过程及寻找新物理信号至关重要。

Method: 采用数值方法求解旋转Kerr黑洞背景下有质量矢量（Proca）场的运动方程，计算灰体因子（greybody factors）及相应的质量与自旋损失函数。通过分析不同极化模式（标量纵模与两个矢量横模）的贡献，并与零质量极限下的已知结果（如标量场和Maxwell场）进行对比验证。

Result: 1) Proca场的标量（纵向）极化模在零质量极限下趋近自由标量场谱，但有限质量时差异显著；2) 两个矢量（横向）极化模在零质量极限下与Page计算的Maxwell场结果一致；3) 慢旋黑洞蒸发由标量模主导，接近极端黑洞时由矢量模主导；4) 霍金温度$T_H\lesssim |μ-Ω_H|$时发射受玻尔兹曼抑制；5) 超辐射放大因子对有质量矢量场达~7%，高于质量为零光子的~4%。

Conclusion: 旋转黑洞可以有效辐射有质量矢量场，即使在霍金温度远低于场质量的情况下，高自旋黑洞仍能高效发射。这一发现对暗物质产生机制、黑洞质量间隙的起源等天体物理现象具有重要启示。同时，有质量矢量场更强的超辐射效应也可能在引力波观测中留下可探测的特征。

Abstract: We compute, for the first time, the Hawking emission spectrum of massive vector (Proca) fields by spinning Kerr black holes, determining the associated greybody factors and the resulting mass and spin loss functions. We show, in particular, that the scalar (longitudinal) polarization of the Proca field has a spectrum approaching that of a free scalar field in the massless limit (in which it becomes a pure gauge mode), although we find substantial differences for finite mass. The contribution of the two vector (transverse) polarization modes coincides, as expected, with the one obtained by Page for the Maxwell field in the massless limit. The black hole's evaporation rate is dominated by the scalar mode for slowly spinning black holes and by the two vector modes as the black hole approaches extremality. As for other fields, we find that Proca Hawking emission is Boltzmann-suppressed for Hawking temperatures $T_H\lesssim |μ-Ω_H|$, where $μ$ is the field mass and $Ω_H$ is the angular velocity of the black hole's horizon. This implies that highly spinning black holes can efficiently emit massive vector fields at temperatures parametrically below the field's mass. Finally, we also find that superradiant emission is more pronounced for massive vector fields, with a maximum amplification factor of $\simeq 7\%$ (compared to $\simeq 4\%$ for massless photons).

</details>


### [161] [Nonradial perturbations of static charged wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18399)
*Jose Luis Blázquez-Salcedo,Luis Manuel González-Romero,Fech Scen Khoo,Jutta Kunz,Pablo Navarro Moreno*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中与鬼标量场最小耦合的静态带电Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的非径向拟正则模谱，发现了带电配置可以显著降低阻尼率，并揭示了非径向极向不稳定性，表明非径向扇区对带电虫洞的动态可行性施加了额外约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带电Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的非径向扰动谱及其稳定性，特别是电荷对阻尼时间和振荡频率的影响，以及是否存在非径向不稳定性。

Method: 推导了轴向和极向扇区的线性扰动方程（包含引力、电磁和鬼标量自由度），采用Chebyshev谱方法计算拟正则模谱。

Result: 无电荷极限再现了已知的Ellis-Bronnikov谱并展示电磁等谱性；电荷可显著降低阻尼率（尤其在极端Reissner-Nordström极限下）；发现了非径向极向不稳定性（基模l=2分支，足够大虫洞质量时最明显）。

Conclusion: 非径向扇区对带电虫洞的动态稳定性施加了额外约束，该不稳定性不同于已知的径向Ellis-Bronnikov不稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate the nonradial quasinormal-mode spectrum of static charged Ellis--Bronnikov wormholes in Einstein--Maxwell theory minimally coupled to a phantom scalar field. The background solutions are known in closed form and comprise three classes: subcritical, critical and supercritical, which all approach the extremal Reissner--Nordström geometry at the boundary of their domain of existence. We derive the linear perturbation equations for axial and polar sectors, including the coupled gravitational, electromagnetic and phantom-scalar degrees of freedom, and compute the corresponding spectra by means of a Chebyshev spectral method. The uncharged limit reproduces the known Ellis--Bronnikov spectrum and exhibits the expected electromagnetic isospectrality. For charged configurations we track the axial and polar branches across the three families of solutions and identify the effect of the charge on the damping times and oscillation frequencies. In particular, we find that charge can substantially reduce damping rates as the extremal Reissner--Nordström limit is approached. We also uncover a nonradial polar instability, most clearly visible in the fundamental $l=2$ branch for sufficiently large wormhole masses. This instability is distinct from the familiar radial Ellis--Bronnikov instability and shows that the nonradial sector imposes additional constraints on the dynamical viability of charged wormholes.

</details>


### [162] [Extreme mass-ratio inspirals into Newtonian Proca stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18405)
*João Bernardo Silva,Richard Brito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Proca星（矢量玻色子星）对内部螺旋小天体扰动的响应，发现牛顿极限下其与标量场玻色子星的能量损失相似（差异最多~20%），但存在中心黑洞时基态构型的轨道能量损失率可比球对称Proca星高一到两个数量级。这为未来引力波探测器探测暗物质结构提供了参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数超轻暗物质分析集中在标量场，而矢量场（Proca星）的研究相对不足。未来引力波探测器可能探测暗物质结构特性，因此需要研究Proca星如何响应内部螺旋小天体的扰动，以填补矢量场暗物质结构研究的空白。

Method: 在牛顿极限下，分别考虑球对称和非球对称基态Proca星，计算内部螺旋小天体的总能量损失，并与标量场玻色子星进行对比分析。

Result: 牛顿水平上，Proca星基态中物体能量损失与标量场对应物相似，相对差异最多~20%；基态构型中存在中心寄生黑洞时，最大轨道能量损失率可比球对称Proca星高一到两个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究需进一步扩展到全相对论框架，预计在该框架下Proca星与标量玻色子星基态的差异将更为显著，这为未来引力波探测暗物质结构提供了重要理论基础和方向。

Abstract: Massive bosonic fields can form self-gravitating solitonic structures, which for vector fields are known as Proca stars. For ultralight fields, these structures can describe the cores of dark matter haloes surrounding the supermassive black holes at the center of galaxies. It has been argued that future gravitational-wave detectors might be able to probe the properties of dark matter structures. However, most of the analyses considering ultralight dark matter have focused on massive scalar fields. In this work, we study how Proca stars respond to a perturbing small object inspiralling in their interior, in the Newtonian limit. We consider both Newtonian spherically symmetric Proca stars and the non-spherically symmetric ground-state solutions. We compute the total energy lost by the orbiting object and compare it to the case where the bosonic star is composed of a scalar field. Our results show that, at the Newtonian level, the energy lost by the object in a Proca star ground-state is similar to that in its scalar field counterpart, with relative differences of at most $\sim 20\%$, while the maximum orbital energy loss rate in the ground-state configuration can be one to two orders of magnitude larger than in the spherically symmetric Proca star, when a central parasitic black hole is present. Our work motivates the need to study extreme mass-ratio inspirals into Proca stars using a fully relativistic setup, where differences between the Proca and scalar boson star ground states are expected to become more significant.

</details>


### [163] [Vacuum Gravity from Entropy: Stability, Spectra, and Exact Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18518)
*David S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了"从熵引出的引力"理论的真空动力学，推导出Minkowski Hessian等价于二次引力作用量的形式，并发现标量模式质量为$m_0^2=3/(5\beta)$，而自旋-2模式具有$m_2^2=-6/(5\beta)$的负质量平方，表明在$\beta>0$时存在快子不稳定性。所有四维Ricci平直度量及平方零Ricci平直pp-波均为精确的局部真空解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解"引力来自熵"这一非传统引力理论的真空动力学性质，特别是其微扰谱与不稳定性特征，以及其与二次引力理论的关系。

Method: 通过对曲率迹在零、一、二形式扇区上的求迹计算，推导Minkowski Hessian；采用对角化曲率块方法简化作用量；线性化G-场方程并施加代数真空约束；分析Ricci平直度量的解性质。

Result: Minkowski Hessian精确等价于二次引力$A R + B R_{\mu\nu}R^{\mu\nu}$，其中$A=3\beta/\ell_P^4$，$B=5\beta^2/(2\ell_P^4)$。谱中包含无质量引力子、标量模$m_0^2=3/(5\beta)$，以及自旋-2模$m_2^2=-6/(5\beta)=-2m_0^2$。$\beta>0$时自旋-2为快子。所有4D Ricci平直度量及平方零Ricci平直pp-波均为精确局部真空解。

Conclusion: "引力从熵"理论在真空动力学上等价于特定的二次引力理论，但存在快子不稳定性问题；该理论的解空间包含所有Ricci平直度量，具有丰富的结构；孤立无质量横向无迹本征空间上的二次平移流具有标准广义相对论归一化。

Abstract: We analyze the vacuum dynamics of Gravity from Entropy, including its algebraically constrained $G$-field formulation. Evaluating the curvature traces over zero-, one-, and two-form sectors, we show that the complete Minkowski Hessian is exactly that of the quadratic-gravity action $A R+B R_{μν}R^{μν}$, with $A=3β/\ell_{\rm P}^{4}$ and $B=5β^{2}/(2\ell_{\rm P}^{4})$. For diagonalizable curvature blocks, the same action reduces to a sum over eigenvalue logarithms and reproduces these coefficients exactly. A strict diagonal-curvature restriction on the perturbations is instead only a reduced subsector and excludes non-diagonalizable type-N wave curvatures. Linearizing the $G$-field equations and subsequently imposing the algebraic vacuum constraint reproduces the same reduced metric equation and covariant Minkowski Hessian. The spectrum contains the massless graviton, a scalar with $m_{0}^{2}=3/(5β)$, and an opposite-residue spin-2 branch with $m_{2}^{2}=-6/(5β)=-2m_{0}^{2}$. For the foundational choice $β>0$, conventional Einstein normalization therefore implies a tachyonic spin-2 instability. We also show that every four-dimensional Ricci-flat metric solves the local bulk equations through quadratic curvature order, while square-zero Ricci-flat pp-waves are exact local vacuum solutions of the analytic metric-only logarithmic branch. On the isolated massless transverse-traceless eigenspace, the quadratic translation current has the standard general-relativistic normalization.

</details>


### [164] [Modified Cosmology from Mass-to-Horizon Relation: Observational Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18521)
*Pranav Prasanthan,Hussain Gohar,Vincenzo Salzano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从广义质量-视界关系出发，约束了基于Cai-Kim视界温度与广义视界熵的修正宇宙学模型。利用多种宇宙学观测数据（Ia超新星、宇宙时钟、重子声学振荡、CMB距离先验）进行MCMC拟合，发现熵指数被严格限制在$|m-1|\lesssim O(10^{-4})$（固定耦合参数时）至$O(10^{-3})$（考虑简并时），排除任何宏观显著偏离标准视界热力学的可能性。释放耦合参数或纠缠振幅可将哈勃常数提升至$h\simeq0.70$-$0.71$，将CMB-SH0ES张力从~4σ降至~1.2-2.6σ，但无法在平坦宇宙中完全解决。贝叶斯对数证据在所有数据组合下均强烈反对扩展模型相对于ΛCDM（$-16\lesssimΔ\ln Z \lesssim-1$），表明含SH0ES时改善的拟合仅是额外参数吸收了哈勃张力，而非修正视界熵的真实证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证从广义质量-视界关系（MHR）推导的修正宇宙学模型是否与观测一致，并检验修正视界熵能否缓解哈勃常数（$H_0$）测量中的CMB-SH0ES张力。

Method: 基于Cai-Kim视界温度与广义视界熵的热力学一致性约束修正弗里德曼方程，引入熵指数$m$、MHR耦合参数$γ$、纠缠修正振幅$f_B$。采用Pantheon+/SH0ES Ia超新星、宇宙时钟（CC）、DESI DR2重子声学振荡（BAO）、Planck 2018 CMB距离先验进行MCMC拟合，通过贝叶斯对数证据$\ln Z$比较模型。

Result: 熵指数$m$被严格约束：固定$γ=1$时$|m-1|\lesssim O(10^{-4})$，考虑$m$-$γ$简并时放宽至$O(10^{-3})$。释放参数后$h$升至0.70-0.71，张力从~4σ降至~1.2-2.6σ。贝叶斯证据强烈反对所有扩展模型（$-16\lesssimΔ\ln Z \lesssim-1$）。

Conclusion: 标准ΛCDM仍是最优模型，修正视界熵无法提供真实证据以解决哈勃张力，观测对标准视界热力学具有高度鲁棒的约束。

Abstract: We constrain the class of modified cosmologies derived in Paper I [1] from a generalized mass-to-horizon relation (MHR) that enforces thermodynamic consistency between the Cai-Kim horizon temperature and generalized horizon entropies. The modified Friedmann equations depend on an entropy exponent $m$, an MHR coupling parameter $γ$, and an entanglement-correction amplitude $f_B$, with standard $Λ$CDM recovered in the appropriate limit. Using Pantheon$+$/SH0ES Type~Ia supernovae, cosmic chronometers, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations, and Planck 2018 CMB distance priors, we constrain eight physically motivated sub-cases via Markov chain Monte Carlo and compare models through the Bayesian log-evidence. The entropy exponent is tightly bounded, $|m-1|\lesssim O(10^{-4})$ when the MHR coupling is fixed ($γ=1$), relaxing to $O(10^{-3})$ along the $m$-$γ$ degeneracy, excluding any macroscopically significant departure from standard horizon thermodynamics. Freeing the MHR coupling parameter or the entanglement amplitude raises the inferred Hubble constant to $h\simeq0.70$-$0.71$, reducing the CMB-SH0ES tension from ${\sim}4σ$ to ${\sim}1.2$-$2.6σ$, but no scenario fully resolves it within a flat universe. The Bayesian log-evidence nevertheless disfavors every extension relative to $Λ$CDM in all dataset combinations ($-16\lesssimΔ\ln Z \lesssim-1$): the improved fits obtained when the SH0ES calibration is included reflect an absorption of the Hubble tension by the additional parameters rather than genuine evidence for modified horizon entropy.

</details>


### [165] [Periodic Orbits and Gravitational Wave Signatures around the Bonanno--Reuter Regular Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18627)
*Mohammad Reza Alipour,Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan,Jafar Sadeghi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Bonanno-Reuter正则黑洞（来自渐近安全引力）中的类时测地线、周期轨道及其引力波信号。参数α/M²控制强场动力学，增大该参数会使束缚轨道内移、相空间收缩。周期轨道发生轻微向内收缩，降低维持特定拓扑所需的能量。数值框架下计算的极端质量比旋近引力波显示：渐近安全修正导致左移相位偏移和峰值振幅增强，高旋绕轨道对修正最敏感，产生与环境效应不同的可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索渐近安全引力修正下的正则黑洞是否会产生不同于经典黑洞的可观测引力波特征，特别是评估其与背景环境效应的区分能力，为LISA、Taiji、天琴等未来空间引力波探测器提供理论预测。

Method: (1) 分析Bonanno-Reuter度规下类时测地线运动方程；(2) 通过有理频率比q=w+v/z对周期轨道分类；(3) 采用数值kludge框架计算极端质量比旋近的引力波偏振波形；(4) 对比不同α/M²参数下的轨道特征和波形差异。

Result: (1) α/M²增大使最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)和边缘束缚轨道向内收缩，半径、角动量和能量均减小；(2) 周期轨道向内收缩，维持特定拓扑所需能量降低；(3) 引力波波形出现左移相位偏移（反映轨道周期缩短）和峰值振幅增强（因近日距更小）；(4) 高旋绕轨道对修正最敏感，偏差最为显著；(5) 该修正不同于膨胀轨道尺度的环境效应，产生独特的可观测特征。

Conclusion: 渐近安全引力修正会在引力波波形中产生独特、可观测的特征信号——左移相位偏移和振幅增强，且与天体物理环境效应截然不同，为未来空间引力波探测器区分量子引力效应与背景环境提供了潜在的诊断工具。

Abstract: Timelike geodesics, periodic orbits, and their associated gravitational-wave signatures are examined in the spacetime of a Bonanno--Reuter regular black hole, a geometry arising from Asymptotically Safe Gravity in which a running Newton coupling replaces the central singularity with a de Sitter core. The dimensionless parameter $α/M^2$ completely determines the strong-field dynamics. Increasing $α/M^2$ shifts the marginally bound and innermost stable circular orbits inward, systematically reducing their characteristic radii, angular momenta, and energies; the allowed phase space for bound motion contracts accordingly. Classifying trajectories via the rational frequency ratio $q = w + v/z$ reveals that periodic orbits experience a mild inward contraction, which reduces the energy necessary to sustain a specific topology. Within the numerical kludge framework, we calculate the gravitational-wave polarizations for extreme mass-ratio inspirals. The asymptotically safe correction induces a leftward phase shift that reflects shorter orbital periods, while mildly enhancing peak amplitudes owing to the smaller periastron distances reached in the deep strong-field regime. Waveform sensitivity displays a strong dependence on topology, with high-whirl orbits, which persist longer in the strong-field region near the horizon, showing markedly more pronounced deviations. Unlike environmental effects that inflate orbital scales, intrinsic quantum-gravity modifications generate distinct, observationally detectable signatures for future space-based detectors such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin.

</details>


### [166] [Stochastic template banks for GW searches using low-discrepancy sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18633)
*Tarun Kumar,Anand S. Sengupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现，在随机模板库生成中使用低差异序列替代均匀随机采样，可在二维和三维参数空间中分别减少27.5%和12%的提议点数量，同时保持相当的恢复率。最终模板数量变化很小（~1%），符合度量-体积约束。主要优势在于降低内存占用和簿记开销，为引力波搜索提供了一种简单可扩展的改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统匹配滤波搜索中，几何、随机和混合模板放置方法在高维参数空间中存在覆盖不均和模板冗余问题。混合方法效率较高但实现复杂，随机方法简单但需大量提议点才能实现充分覆盖。因此，需要一种既能保持随机方法简单性、又能提高采样效率的方法。

Method: 使用低差异序列（low-discrepancy sequences）替代均匀随机采样来生成随机模板库，在二维和三维参数空间中与传统的均匀随机采样方法进行对比，评估其提议点数量、模板计数和恢复率等性能指标。

Result: 低差异序列方法在二维空间中减少27.5%的提议点，三维空间中减少12%的提议点，同时恢复率相当。最终模板数量变化仅~1%，与度量-体积约束一致。实际运行时间加速比提议点减少幅度更温和。

Conclusion: 低差异采样为随机模板库生成提供了一种简单且可扩展的改进方案，主要收益在于降低内存使用和生成过程中的簿记开销，适用于当前和未来的引力波搜索。

Abstract: Matched filtering remains the most sensitive method for detecting gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences. The efficiency of such searches depends on how well a discrete template bank covers the underlying parameter space. Conventional geometric, stochastic, and hybrid placement methods can lead to uneven coverage and redundant templates in higher dimensions. Hybrid methods are generally the most efficient among these, while stochastic methods are simpler to implement, particularly when the parameter-space metric is difficult to compute. In practice, both approaches rely on uniform random sampling, which often requires a large number of proposal points to achieve adequate coverage. We find that stochastic template banks constructed using low-discrepancy sequences achieve comparable recovery fractions while requiring 27.5\% fewer proposal points in two dimensions and 12\% fewer in three dimensions. The final template count changes only marginally ($\sim 1\%$), consistent with the metric-volume constraints of the covering problem. The primary benefit of low-discrepancy sampling is therefore a reduction in the size of the initial proposal set, leading to lower memory usage and reduced bookkeeping during bank generation. Since the final template count is governed mainly by the metric volume of the target parameter space, the wall-clock speed-up is more modest than the reduction in proposal count. Nevertheless, low-discrepancy sampling provides a simple and scalable improvement to stochastic template-bank generation for current and future gravitational-wave searches.

</details>


### [167] [Scale-invariant Schrödinger geometry in symmetric teleparallel gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18657)
*Wei-Zhi Liu,Jing-Ying Wang,Shi-Dong Liang,Hong-Hao Zhang,Tiberiu Harko,Lei Ming*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称遥平行引力中构建了薛定谔几何的局域尺度不变表述，识别出保持自平行性、无挠性和薛定谔仿射结构的唯一尺度变换，并基于此得到了二次型尺度不变作用量。利用Palatini formalism证明了尺度不变性所需的条件恰好确保仿射联络被动力学约化为薛定谔形式，从而建立了局域尺度对称性与保长仿射几何之间的直接对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索尺度不变性在度规-仿射引力中的几何基础，建立局域尺度对称性与薛定谔几何（一种保长仿射几何）之间的严格对应关系，为尺度不变的度规-仿射引力提供新的几何框架。

Method: 首先识别保持自平行性、无挠性和薛定谔仿射结构的唯一尺度变换；利用该变换构造二次型尺度不变作用量；采用Palatini formalism（将度规和联络视为独立变量）分析动力学约化条件，证明尺度不变性自动导致仿射联络取薛定谔形式。

Result: 找到了保持薛定谔几何基本结构（自平行性、无挠性、薛定谔仿射结构）的唯一尺度变换；基于此变换可构造二次型尺度不变作用量；证明了使用Palatini formalism时，尺度不变性条件等价于仿射联络动力学约化为薛定谔联络形式的条件。

Conclusion: 局域尺度对称性与薛定谔几何的保长仿射结构之间存在直接的数学对应关系，这一发现为构建尺度不变的度规-仿射引力理论提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: We construct a locally scale-invariant formulation of Schrödinger geometry in symmetric teleparallel gravity. The unique scale transformation preserving autoparallelism, torsionlessness and the Schrödinger affine structure is first identified, from which a quadratic scale-invariant action is obtained. Using the Palatini formalism, we show that the conditions required for scale invariance are exactly those ensuring that the affine connection is dynamically reduced to the Schrödinger form. Our results establish a direct correspondence between local scale symmetry and length-preserving affine geometry, providing a new geometric framework for scale-invariant metric-affine gravity.

</details>


### [168] [Novel extended inner shadow in images of Johannsen-Psaltis black holes with thin accretion disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18751)
*Xinyu Wang,Xiaobao Wang,Xin-Yang Wang,Minyong Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用数值反向光线追踪模拟，系统研究了Johannsen-Psaltis (JP)度规中带有偏差参数的黑洞成像特征（包括图像结构、强度分布、红移-蓝移信号及内阴影），特别分析了非闭合事件视界JP黑洞中出现的扩展内阴影及其物理形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JP度规通过引入偏差参数扩展了Kerr度规，为检验天体物理黑洞是否符合Kerr范式及无毛定理提供了额外自由度。黑洞成像是探测黑洞性质最直接的手段之一，因此系统研究JP黑洞的成像特征是运用该度规探索偏离Kerr几何的第一步。

Method: 采用数值反向光线追踪模拟，并结合近似解析框架，对具有闭合与非闭合事件视界的JP黑洞进行成像特征（图像结构、强度分布、红移-蓝移特征）的系统分析，特别针对非闭合视界情形的扩展内阴影进行成因探究。

Result: 系统揭示了JP黑洞成像特征随偏差参数的变化规律；在非闭合事件视界的JP黑洞图像中发现了与原始内阴影不同的扩展内阴影现象。

Conclusion: JP度规下的黑洞成像存在显著的偏差参数依赖性，非闭合视界JP黑洞中出现的扩展内阴影为检验Kerr几何偏离提供了新型可观测特征；数值模拟与解析框架相结合的方法可有效解释其物理形成机制。

Abstract: The Johannsen-Psaltis (JP) metric is constructed by introducing deviation parameters into the Kerr metric. The presence of these deviation parameters provides additional degrees of freedom for testing the extent to which astrophysical black holes are consistent with the Kerr paradigm and for examining the validity of the no-hair theorem. Since black hole imaging provides one of the most direct means of probing the properties of astrophysical black holes, a detailed investigation of the imaging characteristics of JP black holes constitutes a natural first step toward employing the JP metric to explore potential deviations from the Kerr geometry in astrophysical black hole systems. Accordingly, numerical backward ray-tracing simulations are employed to conduct a comprehensive investigation of the image structures, intensity distributions, and redshift-blueshift signatures of JP black holes with both closed and non-closed event horizons. The dependence of these imaging characteristics on the deviation parameter is also investigated systematically. Furthermore, the emergence of an extended inner shadow, distinct from the original inner shadow of the black hole, in images of JP black holes with non-closed event horizons motivated a detailed investigation of its physical formation mechanism using both numerical backward ray-tracing simulations and an approximate analytical framework.

</details>


### [169] [From gravastar to central singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18764)
*G. E. Volovik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了规则黑洞模型，证明引力星在热力学上不稳定，倾向于演化为带有奇点的史瓦西黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨引力星（gravastar）作为黑洞奇点替代方案的热力学稳定性问题。

Method: 基于规则黑洞模型，通过热力学分析比较引力星与史瓦西黑洞的稳定性。

Result: 引力星在热力学上是不稳定的，会向带有奇点的史瓦西黑洞演化。

Conclusion: 引力星作为无奇点黑洞替代模型在热力学层面存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: We consider the model of the regular black hole, which demonstrates that the gravastar is thermodynamically unstable towards the Schwarzschild black hole with singularity.

</details>


### [170] [Tidal Deformability of Neutron Stars in Bumblebee Gravity: Probing Lorentz Symmetry Breaking with Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18812)
*Ayan Banerjee,sherzod Sayfiyev,Muhtarama Radjapbaeva,Javlon Rayimbaev,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Aseel Smerat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在蜂鸟引力理论的Neves-Gardim分支中研究了静态中子星的潮汐性质，通过有效度规变形方法计算了四极潮汐Love数和无量纲潮汐形变参数，并利用GW170817事件对理论参数ℓ给出了依赖于物态方程的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自发破缺洛伦兹对称性的矢量-张量理论（蜂鸟引力）中，静态中子星分支的完整耦合微扰推导尚不可用，因此需要发展一种有效方法来研究该理论下中子星的潮汐性质，并利用引力波观测进行参数约束。

Method: 基于修正的TOV方程组（保留含m''(r)的项），将系统重写为代数等价的一阶形式；采用有效度规变形方案，在修改后的蜂鸟背景上评估Hinderer流体微扰方程，保留标准表面匹配关系，计算了四种物态方程在ℓ∈[-0.4,+1.0]范围内的k₂和Λ。

Result: 广义相对论极限（ℓ=0）的基准计算误差在0.2%以内；与GW170817的潮汐形变界Λ̃≤720对比，得到物态方程依赖的参数上限：BSk20（ℓ_max=+0.25）、BSk21（+0.06）、DD2（-0.09）、MS1（-0.25）。

Conclusion: 所得潮汐限制依赖于所采用的微扰方案，待完整的线性化矢量-张量问题可解时需重新评估。

Abstract: We investigate the tidal properties of static neutron stars in the Neves--Gardim branch of bumblebee gravity, a vector--tensor theory in which a nonzero vacuum expectation value of the bumblebee field spontaneously breaks local Lorentz symmetry. The stellar background is determined from the full modified Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff system, including the term containing $m''(r)$. For a differentiable barotropic equation of state, this system is recast into an algebraically equivalent first-order form without discarding any bumblebee-dependent contribution. Because a complete coupled derivation of static even-parity metric and bumblebee-field perturbations is not presently available for this stellar branch, the tidal sector is treated through an explicit effective metric-deformation prescription: the Hinderer fluid perturbation equation is evaluated on the modified bumblebee background, and the standard surface matching relation is retained as part of the same prescription. We compute the quadrupolar tidal Love number $k_2$ and the dimensionless tidal deformability $Λ=(2/3)k_2\mathcal{C}^{-5}$ for the BSk20, BSk21, DD2, and MS1 equations of state over $\ell\in[-0.4,+1.0]$. The general-relativistic limit is recovered at $\ell=0$ to within $0.2\%$ in the benchmark calculations. Within the adopted effective prescription, comparison with the GW170817 binary tidal-deformability bound $\widetildeΛ\leq720$ yields the EOS-dependent limiting values $\ell_{\max}=+0.25$ (BSk20), $+0.06$ (BSk21), $-0.09$ (DD2), and $-0.25$ (MS1). These tidal limits are conditional on the stated perturbative prescription and should be reassessed when the complete linearised vector--tensor problem becomes available.

</details>


### [171] [Defrosting the Born-Infeld dyonic frozen star with tachyon matter: spectrum of oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18851)
*Ram Brustein,A. J. M. Medved,Tamar Simhon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究"冻星"（frozen star）模型——一种无奇点、无陷获面的黑洞内部替代模型，通过引入微扰磁性单极子和快子动能实现"解冻"，并基于Born-Infeld拉格朗日量推导出与非径向振荡一致的频谱，验证了声速和寿命的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻星作为无奇点黑洞内部模型具有超稳定性，但需要引入微扰使其能够产生内部脉动（即"解冻"），以符合正则、无视界的天体物理黑洞预期行为。此前基于流体力学的分析需要更简洁、普适的推导方法。

Method: 基于Born-Infeld拉格朗日量直接推导冻星解冻后的扰动方程，避免了此前流体方法的复杂性，并验证了非径向振荡频谱。通过引入微扰参数γ描述磁性单极子电荷和快子动能的影响。

Result: 声速标度为γ，参量化长寿命标度为1/γ²；扰动方程由于源自拉格朗日量，其一致性在任意偏离球对称的形变下得到保证，有利于非平衡态数值研究。

Conclusion: 基于Born-Infeld的拉格朗日方法比流体方法更简洁且一致性更好，为冻星模型的数值模拟和进一步理论研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The frozen star is a model for the interior of an astrophysical black hole that is externally indistinguishable from the singular geometry of a Schwarzschild black hole, even though it contains no singularities nor trapped surfaces. The frozen star corresponds to a macroscopic ``BIon'', a solution of a certain type of Born-Infeld Lagrangian coupled to Einstein gravity, which consists of rigid flux tubes sourced by an electric charge localized at its center and a uniform distribution of opposite charge along its outer surface. This configuration is a solution of the effective action that describes the end point of tachyon condensation in string. The rigidity of the flux tubes implies that the star is ultrastable under linear perturbations of the geometry and matter. The star can be effectively deformed, or ``defrosted'', allowing internal pulsations, as expected from regular, horizonless astrophysical black holes. To describe the defrosted star, we need to include a perturbative amount of magnetic-monopole charge and tachyon kinetic energy. This recasts the star as a macroscopic ``DIon'', whose flux tubes can stretch and contract, having both electric and magnetic charges localized at its center and distributed uniformly along its outer surface. Previously, the non-radial oscillations of the defrosted star were analyzed to leading order in a perturbative ``defrosting'' parameter $γ$, by describing the energy-momentum tensor as that of a fluid. Here, we derive the same spectrum in a much simpler way, using the Born-Infeld Lagrangian. Thus, we verify that the sound velocities scale as $γ$ and the parametrically long lifetimes scale as $1/γ^2$. Because the perturbation equations are derived from a Lagrangian, their consistency is guaranteed for arbitrary deviations away from spherical symmetry, facilitating numerical studies on frozen stars away from equilibrium.

</details>


### [172] [On Generalized (Conformal) Killing Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18871)
*Cynthia Arias,David Kopčan,David Kubiznak,Marek Milička*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究具有混合对称性的高阶广义Killing张量，证明其在高维旋转黑洞时空中自然存在，并提出沿零测地线平行传输的张量推广形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有混合对称性的高阶广义Killing张量的存在性及其在广义相对论和更高维引力理论中的几何与物理意义。

Method: 通过Killing-Yano张量的部分收缩“平方”构造高阶广义Killing张量，分析其代数与微分性质；并受共形Killing张量启发，提出沿零测地线平行传输的张量推广方案。

Result: 1. 具有混合对称性的高阶广义Killing张量在高维旋转黑洞时空中自然存在；2. 这些对象展现出有趣的代数与微分性质；3. 所提出的沿零测地线平行传输的张量推广形式同样存在于高维旋转黑洞时空中。

Conclusion: 具有混合对称性的高阶广义Killing张量是高维旋转黑洞时空的重要几何结构，其推广形式为进一步研究测地线运动和可积性提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study higher-rank generalized Killing tensors with mixed symmetries, providing a couple of examples and applications. It is shown that such objects naturally exist in higher-dimensional rotating black hole spacetimes, where they arise as partially contracted "squares" of Killing-Yano tensors and display interesting algebraic and differential properties. Motivated by conformal Killing tensors, a generalization of these objects that gives rise to parallel-transported tensors along null geodesics is proposed and shown to exist in higher-dimensional rotating black hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [173] [Quasinormal Ringdown and Echoes in Accreting Exotic Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18919)
*Chanchal Sharma,Kabir Chakravarti,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了吸积过程中致密天体向黑洞转变时引力波回波信号的压缩、抑制及最终消失的过程，表明回波消失可作为视界形成的特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  sufficiently紧凑的奇异致密天体在经历现实吸积后可能最终形成事件视界，但此前对吸积过程中引力波回波如何演化尚不清楚，理解这一转变过程对于区分黑洞与致密天体至关重要。

Method: 采用内行Vaidya解对动态时空背景建模，并数值演化标量扰动，模拟从反射表面到事件视界形成的连续过渡过程。

Result: 吸积过程中回波信号被逐渐压缩和抑制，当增长的事件视界吞没反射表面时回波完全消失，波形平滑过渡到标准黑洞ringdown。

Conclusion: 引力波回波的消失可作为视界形成的特征信号，为未来引力波观测研究动态致密天体提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Exotic compact objects (ECOs) may produce late-time gravitational-wave (GW) echoes, but sufficiently compact ECOs undergoing realistic accretion may eventually form an event horizon. We investigate the evolution of quasinormal modes and GW echoes during this transition. Modeling the exterior spacetime with the ingoing Vaidya solution, we numerically evolve scalar perturbations on the resulting dynamical background. We show that the echo signal is progressively compressed and suppressed during accretion and disappears once the growing event horizon engulfs the reflecting surface, after which the waveform smoothly approaches the standard black hole ringdown. Our results identify the disappearance of GW echoes as a characteristic signature of horizon formation and provide a framework for studying dynamical compact objects with future GW observations.

</details>


### [174] [Accretion of multipolar massive complex scalar field packets by a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18920)
*Flavio Rosales-Infante,Ivan Alvarez-Rios,Francisco S. Guzman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超轻模糊暗物质背景下，Schwarzschild黑洞对复杂大标量波包的有限时标吸积过程。通过分解Klein-Gordon场为独立多极扇区并逐模演化高斯波包，利用Noether守恒流计算黑洞吸收的标量电荷。构建了吸积效率图，揭示了吸积由低效到高效的转变，并证明该过程受黑洞视界半径与约化Compton波长之比控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定局域标量波包与黑洞相互作用后其标量含量如何重新分布，以及哪些谱分量和多极分量更易被黑洞吸收。

Method: 将Klein-Gordon场分解为独立多极扇区，逐模演化近单色高斯波包，将问题简化为1+1维演化；利用穿过视界面的Noether守恒流定量吸积。

Result: 构建了(k₀,ℓ)平面内的吸积效率图，展示了从低效到部分再到高效吸积的转变；发现该过程由Schwarzschild半径R_s与约化Compton波长λ̄_C之比控制：R_s≲λ̄_C时转变宽且受角动量势垒主导，R_s>λ̄_C时转变尖锐且低k₀区出现部分吸积平台。

Conclusion: 提供了一种基于时间域和Noether电荷的黑洞对标量波包吸积的分类方法。

Abstract: We study the finite-time accretion of complex massive scalar wave packets by a Schwarzschild black hole in the test-field regime, with parameters motivated by ultralight fuzzy dark matter around supermassive black holes. Our goal is to determine how the scalar content of a localized configuration is redistributed after interacting with the black hole, and which spectral and multipolar components are more efficiently absorbed. We decompose the Klein--Gordon field into independent multipolar sectors and evolve nearly monochromatic Gaussian packets mode by mode, reducing the problem to a set of 1+1 dimensional evolutions. Accretion is quantified with the flux of the conserved Noether current through the horizon surface, providing a direct measure of the scalar charge absorbed by the black hole. For a carrier radial wavenumber $k_0$ and multipole index $\ell$, we construct accretion-efficiency maps in the $(k_0,\ell)$ plane that contain the fraction of accreted modal charge. These maps exhibit a transition between inefficient, partial, and efficient accretion regimes, which we relate to the structure of an effective potential. We show that the process is controlled by the ratio between the Schwarzschild radius $R_s$ and the reduced Compton wavelength $\lambdabar_C$. For $R_s \lesssim \lambdabar_C$, the transition is broad and dominated by the angular momentum barrier, while for $R_s > \lambdabar_C$ it sharpens across a narrower range of $k_0$ and a partial-accretion floor emerges at low $k_0$. These results provide a time-domain, Noether-charge-based classification of black hole accretion for massive scalar wave packets.

</details>


### [175] [Gravity-Induced Entanglement of Quantum Clocks as a Signature of Genuinely Quantum Local Position Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19141)
*Eyuri Wakakuwa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了量子时钟的引力诱导纠缠（GIE）为检验量子引力区域中的局域位置不变性（LPI）提供了框架，并提出了两种方案来区分量子与经典LPI违背的不同表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论原理（特别是等效原理）能否在量子引力区域中成立是现代物理学的一个开放问题。

Method: 将量子LPI的现有表述适配到GIE框架中，通过选择不同初始时钟状态设计两种互补方案：一种仅在量子LPI违背时产生纠缠，另一种则通过纠缠振荡频率反映经典类LPI违背。

Result: 两种方案分别对应LPI的不同方面，能够在GIE框架下区分量子与经典LPI违背。

Conclusion: 量子时钟的GIE为在量子区域中研究广义相对论基本原理提供了新途径，为量子力学与引力理论的交汇带来新见解。

Abstract: Whether the principles of general relativity extend to a quantum regime remains one of the open questions in modern physics. In classical general relativity, Einstein's equivalence principle underpins the interpretation of gravity as spacetime geometry. However, it is not known whether the principle remains valid when the source of gravity could be quantum. Here we show that gravity-induced entanglement (GIE) of quantum clocks provides a framework to characterize local position invariance (LPI) in such a regime, which constitutes one of the subprinciples of the equivalence principle. By adapting the existing formulation of quantum LPI to the context of GIE, we analyze two schemes that address complementary aspects of LPI and differ only in the choice of the initial clock state: one in which entanglement will be generated if and only if there is a genuinely quantum violation of LPI, and the other where classical-like LPI violation manifests in the frequency of entanglement oscillation. Our results suggest that GIE of quantum clocks offers an approach to investigating fundamental principles of general relativity in the quantum regime, shedding new light on the interplay between quantum mechanics and the theory of gravity.

</details>


### [176] [A wormhole with two black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19145)
*Gérard Clément*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文报告了四维真空爱因斯坦方程的一个稳态解，该解描述了一个洛伦兹虫洞，虫洞内嵌有两个对跖共转黑洞，这两个黑洞由两条半无限宇宙弦（张力可正可负）悬挂在“另一个”类空无穷远处。该度规在几乎所有参数取值下都存在一个裸环奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索爱因斯坦场方程中同时包含虫洞、黑洞和宇宙弦的复杂时空结构，寻找具有物理意义的新解。

Method: 通过求解四维真空爱因斯坦方程，构造稳态解，分析其几何与拓扑性质。

Result: 得到了一个描述洛伦兹虫洞的稳态解，虫洞内有两个共转黑洞，由宇宙弦悬挂；该解几乎在所有参数下存在裸环奇点。

Conclusion: 该解展示了一种新颖的时空构型，将虫洞、旋转黑洞和宇宙弦统一在同一框架中，但裸奇点的存在可能影响其物理合理性。

Abstract: We report on a stationary solution of the four-dimensional vacuum Einstein equations, which describes a Lorentzian wormhole harboring two antipodal co-rotating black holes. These are hung from the ``other'' spacelike infinity by two semi-infinite cosmic strings with positive or negative tension. The metric also admits a naked ring singularity for almost all values of the parameters.

</details>


### [177] [Plasma-Induced Modifications of the Shadows of Rotating Bardeen Black Holes with Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19152)
*Gowtham Sidharth M,Sanjit Das*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在完美流体暗物质（PFDM）背景下的旋转正则Bardeen黑洞，在等离子体介质中的光学外观和阴影特征。分析了三种等离子体模型，并利用事件视界望远镜（EHT）的观测约束对等离子体和PFDM参数进行了限定。结果表明，PFDM和等离子体环境效应会对黑洞阴影产生可测量的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨旋转正则Bardeen黑洞在等离子体和PFDM环境中的光学外观，以及如何利用黑洞成像约束周围介质和黑洞本身的性质。

Method: 研究了三种等离子体模型（均匀分布、径向变化分布、径向和角度依赖的一般分布），分析等离子体对光子运动和阴影形态的影响，并使用EHT的阴影圆度和分数直径偏差约束对参数进行限制。

Result: PFDM和等离子体的环境效应对黑洞阴影产生可测量的修正，且黑洞成像可为周围介质和旋转正则黑洞固有性质提供约束。

Conclusion: 黑洞阴影观测能够有效约束黑洞周围的暗物质和等离子体环境参数，旋转正则Bardeen黑洞模型在EHT约束下具有天体物理可行性。

Abstract: We study the optical appearance of a rotating regular Bardeen black hole embedded in perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) when photon propagation occurs through a plasma medium. Three plasma models are examined: a homogeneous distribution, a radially varying distribution, and a general distribution with both radial and angular dependence. The influence of plasma on photon motion and the resulting shadow morphology is analysed using shadow observables. To assess astrophysical viability, the plasma and PFDM parameters are constrained using the Event Horizon Telescope bounds on shadow circularity and fractional diameter deviation. The results demonstrate that environmental effects arising from both PFDM and plasma produce measurable modifications to the shadow, indicating that black-hole imaging can provide useful constraints on the surrounding medium as well as the intrinsic properties of regular rotating black holes.

</details>


### [178] [Calibration of the AdvancedVirgo+ Gravitational Wave Detector and Reconstruction of the Detector Strain h(t) during the Observing Run O4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19273)
*Virgo Collaboration*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文描述了AdvancedVirgo+探测器在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA O4观测运行期间的校准和应变数据重建过程，包括在线和离线数据生成、频率相关不确定度估计（振幅2-3%，相位<30 mrad），以及这些数据通过GWOSC公开发布的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdvancedVirgo+探测器在O4观测运行期间参与引力波探测网络，虽然灵敏度有限（约55 Mpc），但其加入能提高源参数估计精度，特别是天空定位。因此需要对其探测器的应变数据h(t)进行精确校准和重建，以满足后续数据分析需求。

Method: 基于辅助执行器、牛顿校准器和光子校准器的探测器校准方法；在线实时数据处理（延迟约10秒）结合线性噪声减法进行h(t)重建；离线生成AnalysisReady应变数据，并更新频率依赖的不确定度估计。

Result: O4b和O4c期间在线探测到约173个瞬态引力波源（双致密星并合）；应变数据在10-2000 Hz频段振幅不确定度约2-3%，相位不确定度低于30 mrad，但在50 Hz和150 Hz附近不确定度较大；数据已通过GWOSC公开发布。

Conclusion: AdvancedVirgo+的校准和应变数据重建方法成功支撑了O4观测运行，所生成的AnalysisReady应变数据已用于LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA离线数据分析，并通过GWOSC向科学界公开。

Abstract: From 10 April 2024 15:00 UTC to 18 November 2025 16:00 UTC, the AdvancedVirgo+ gravitational wave detector participated in the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA O4 observing run, started on 24 May 2023 15:00 UTC. Around 173 transient gravitational wave (GW) sources, all corresponding to coalescences of binary compact objects involving black holes and neutron stars, were detected online during the two run periods O4b and O4c when Virgo was taking data in the detector network. Despite its sensitivity being limited around 55 Mpc, the inclusion of Virgo into the network allowed to improve the accuracy of the source parameter estimation, in particular the sky localisation of the detected events. This article describes the AdvancedVirgo+ detector calibration and the reconstruction of the detector strain h(t) during O4, as well as the estimation of the associated frequency-dependent uncertainties. The detector calibration is based on auxiliary actuators, Newtonian Calibrators and Photon Calibrators, described in other publications. The h(t) reconstruction, including linear noise subtraction, was processed online with a latency of about 10 s. The so-called AnalysisReady strain data were then produced offline. Most of the time, the strain time series was a copy of the online time series, but with updated frequency-dependent uncertainties, around 2-3% in amplitude and below 30 mrad in phase in the 10-2000 Hz frequency band, with the exception of larger uncertainties around 50 Hz and 150 Hz. The AnalysisReady strain data and associated uncertainties have been used for the offline LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA data analysis and are also the data made publicly available through the Gravitational Wave Open Science Center (GWOSC).

</details>

Zhao,Yue Zhao,Yuhang Zhao,L. -M. Zheng,Y. Zheng,L. Zhizhong,H. Zhong,H. Zhou,H. O. Zhu,X. -J. Zhu,Z. -H. Zhu,Z. Zhu,D. Z. Zieba,A. B. Zimmerman,L. Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA引力波探测器网络第五次引力波瞬变目录（GWTC-5.0）中的168个双星并合事件，对广义相对论（GR）在强场、动力学区域进行了七项系统检验。结果表明：残差与探测器噪声一致，未发现GR预测之外的额外极化模式，对GR后牛顿系数的偏差约束提升了1.2-2.6倍，且剩余黑洞的检验结果与GR整体一致。整体上未发现超越GR的新物理迹象。
